Method: 构建围绕一家完整虚构公司的基准：由行业、规模、商业模式、应用组合和随机种子定义公司；用同一个带种子的实体图向 Salesforce、Zendesk、Slack、Gong 等产品模拟器提供共享数据；再由问题条件生成器生成内部数据库的 schema 与记录，并复用该图中的共享实体、键和值，从而保证整个企业数据一致。答案由最终记录计算得出，实现精确评分，并通过设计与答案键检查验证数据库、用真实性评分卡与对抗检测器验证实体图。

Result: 在 23 家生成公司上，平均真实性得分从 61.8 提升到 97.0，无记录被判定为合成。在模拟器赛道对比中，9 个模型对同一 33 道题各作答 3 次，准确率估计在 42.4% 至 76.8% 之间；36 组两两差异中仅 3 组在校正后仍显著。

Conclusion: 该基准提供了数据一致、答案可精确计算的企业级 LLM 智能体评测方案，并能有效区分模型表现，但当前多数模型间差异有限。

Abstract: LLM agents for enterprise systems of record cannot be evaluated on customer production data, and no existing substitute provides ground truth. We present the Era by Eon Benchmark for evaluating LLM agents that use enterprise tools. The benchmark is built around a complete fictional company. It includes product simulators, company-specific internal databases, benchmark questions, and computed answer keys. Industry, company size, business model, application portfolio, and a seed define each company. One seeded entity graph supplies shared company data to simulators of Salesforce, Zendesk, Slack, Gong, and other products. A questionconditioned generator creates the schemas and records for internal databases. It takes shared entities, keys, and values from the same graph before generating database-specific facts. Both mechanisms therefore describe one consistent enterprise estate. Every expected answer is computed from the final records, so grading is exact. Design and answer-key checks validate the internal databases. A realism scorecard and adversarial detector validate the entity graph. Across 23 generated companies, the mean realism score rose from 61.8 to 97.0, with zero records flagged as synthetic. In the reported simulator-track comparison, nine models answered the same 33 questions three times each. Accuracy estimates ranged from 42.4% to 76.8%, and three of 36 pairwise differences remained supported after correction.

</details>


### [177] [Shifting Relational Paradigms for Affective Computing: Affective Resonance, Vitality Affects, and Vocal Interaction Fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09864)
*Cy Gorman,Yihang Yao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出情感计算应从个体状态范式转向"关系性"框架，以交互场（发声动态中形成）为情感分析的基本单元，并通过自监督语音表示检测多方对话中的方向性表达耦合，为"人工情感共振智能"提供设计与分析框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有情感计算多遵循个体状态范式，从孤立说话人中提取离散情感标签或唤醒/效价，作者认为这一框架无法充分刻画交互情境中的情感现象。基于情感共振与生命性轮廓（vitality-contour）理论，论文主张情感分析的基本单位应是声音动态中构成的"交互场"。

Method: 作为概念验证，作者开展了一项初步实证研究，使用连续的自监督语音表示，在多方对话中检测方向性的表达耦合（directional expressive coupling），并设置"仅语音"负对照（exclusive-speech negative controls）进行零校准（null-calibrated）分析，考察不同交互情境下的耦合模式。

Result: 耦合具有情境/机制特异性，集中在亚秒级时间尺度上，并在负对照条件下消失（collapse），这与情感动态的关系性解释相一致。

Conclusion: 论文提出以"情感共振动态本体论"（Affective Resonance Dynamic Ontologies）为基础的设计框架，面向"人工情感共振智能"（Artificial Affective Resonance Intelligence），并配套跨交互情境的零校准方向性耦合分析，为关系性情感计算提供概念与方法的初步基础。

Abstract: Affective computing has largely followed an individual-state paradigm, extracting discrete emotion labels or arousal/valence from isolated speakers. We argue this framing is incomplete for interaction. Drawing on affective resonance and vitality-contour accounts, we propose a relational framework in which the primary unit of affective analysis is the interactional field constituted within vocal dynamics. As a proof of concept, we present a preliminary empirical study using continuous self-supervised speech representations to detect directional expressive coupling in multi-party conversation. Coupling is regime-specific, concentrated at sub-second timescales, and collapses under exclusive-speech negative controls, consistent with a relational account of affective dynamics. We introduce design frameworks for Artificial Affective Resonance Intelligence grounded in Affective Resonance Dynamic Ontologies, supported by null-calibrated directional coupling analyses across interaction regimes.

</details>


### [178] [AgentAudit: An Open, Extensible Framework for Full-Lifecycle Trust Evaluation of AI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09875)
*Shrey Nag,Sachita,Abhishek Kumar Singh,Lipi Goel,Rajeshwar Singh Janwar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AgentAudit是一个对LLM智能体全执行轨迹进行十维度评估的框架，可定位失败发生的具体阶段，并揭示任务完成度相近的模型在可信度上存在显著差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评估框架大多只考察AI智能体的单一侧面，如任务完成度（AgentBench）或安全鲁棒性（AgentDojo、ASB），无法覆盖规划、工具选择、工具执行、记忆与推理的完整流程。由于失败可能发生在任意阶段，而现有基准很少能识别失败的确切来源，因此需要一个能评估全流程并定位失败阶段的方案。

Method: AgentAudit评估完整执行轨迹，涵盖十项能力、接地、安全与行为维度（指令完整性、规划器、记忆、工具选择、工具调用、工具正确性、对齐、工具忠实性、安全、执行完整性），并结合行为分类与失败归因定位失败阶段。其采用附加式设计而非替换智能体，仅读取记录的执行轨迹、不干预智能体运行，因此对内部实现无约束，可评估任意基于LLM的智能体。实验在九个能力与对抗任务上评估了五个语言模型（OpenAI GPT-5、Claude Sonnet 5、Sarvam 105B、Llama 3.3 70B、Gemini 2.5 Flash）。

Result: Claude Sonnet 5与GPT-5取得最高的平均综合信任分（分别为95.1和80.6，满分100），Sarvam 105B、Llama 3.3 70B和Gemini 2.5 Flash明显落后（分别为57.6、45.7和22.6）。所有轨迹由单一固定评判模型打分，而该模型本身即为被评估模型之一，这一局限在第七.E节中讨论。

Conclusion: 任务完成行为相近的模型在可信度上可能差异巨大：若干非前沿模型在对抗任务中反复被归类为"不安全合规"，而非仅仅任务失败，这是通过/失败型基准无法揭示的区别。AgentAudit提供了覆盖全流程、可归因失败阶段的细粒度智能体评估路径。

Abstract: Existing evaluation frameworks mostly assess only one part of AI agents, such as task completion (AgentBench) or security robustness (AgentDojo, ASB), rather than the complete pipeline of planning, tool selection, tool execution, memory and reasoning. Failures can occur at any stage, yet existing benchmarks rarely identify their precise source. AgentAudit evaluates the entire execution trace across ten capability, grounding, security and behavioural dimensions, namely instruction integrity, planner, memory, tool selection, tool invocation, tool correctness, alignment, tool faithfulness, security and execution integrity, combined with behavioural classification and failure attribution to pinpoint the exact stage responsible for an observed failure. AgentAudit can evaluate any LLM-based AI agent, since it attaches to the agent instead of replacing it. It reads only the recorded execution trace and does not interfere with how the agent runs, so it places no constraint on the agent's internal implementation. We evaluate five language models (OpenAI GPT-5, Claude Sonnet 5, Sarvam 105B, Llama 3.3 70B and Gemini 2.5 Flash) across nine capability and adversarial tasks. Claude Sonnet 5 and GPT-5 obtain the highest mean Composite Trust Scores (95.1 and 80.6 out of 100, respectively), while Sarvam 105B, Llama 3.3 70B and Gemini 2.5 Flash trail substantially (57.6, 45.7 and 22.6). All traces were scored by a single fixed judge model, which was itself one of the evaluated models, a limitation discussed in Section VII.E. More importantly, models with similar task-completion behaviour can diverge sharply in trustworthiness, as several non-frontier models are repeatedly classified Unsafe_Compliance on adversarial tasks rather than merely failing them, a distinction that pass/fail benchmarks cannot surface.

</details>


### [179] [Scored vs. Generated Readouts in Behavioral Language Models: An Empirical Study of Elicitation Format](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09882)
*Touchapon Kraisingkorn,Krittin Pachtrachai,Wachiravit Modecrua*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 在固定模型检查点与提示内容的情况下，比较"打分答案token得到的概率"与"先写理由再生成的预测"两种读出方式，发现前者在13个"模型-领域"单元中有12个的排序准确率更高（AUC高出1.5–14.5个百分点）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型在客户行为预测中既能输出结果也能生成解释，但学界与业界常把这两类读出（概率打分与生成式理由/预测）视为可互换，缺乏对二者实际差异的严格比较与机制分析。

Method: 固定模型检查点与提示内容，在四个零售任务、三个市场共13个"模型-领域"单元（含两个使用完全公开数据与检查点）中对比：(1) 对答案token打分的概率读出；(2) 先生成书面理由再给出预测的读出；并引入第三种读出——在任何判定前先引出概率。采用AUC、配对bootstrap置信区间、双尾符号检验，并分析约9000条理由文本。

Result: 打分读出在12/13个单元中排序更准（符号检验p≈0.003），AUC高出1.5–14.5个百分点，新测单元中配对bootstrap置信区间均不含零；差距随任务特定监督强度和训练-服务格式不匹配而变化，从未微调基座模型的-2.2分到理由格式监督的+13.7分。约9000条理由的分析显示两个相关因素：对主导预测特征的依赖降低、以及趋向套话化表达；概率饱和与差距无关。第三种读出在判定前引出概率可改善校准（Brier从0.47降至0.15），排序与打分相比在噪声范围内，但仅在训练中出现的结局比例下有效，且在打分头已校准的情况下更差。

Conclusion: 两种读出并不等价，差异可用各自所匹配的目标函数来解释；作者指出可缩小差距的训练选择，并建议保留生成的理由，但排序结果应取自打分头。

Abstract: Language models fine-tuned on customer behavior can predict outcomes and generate explanations, but these readouts are often treated as interchangeable. Holding model checkpoint and prompt content fixed, we compare probabilities obtained by scoring answer tokens with predictions generated after a written rationale. Across 13 model-domain cells covering four retail tasks in three markets, including two using fully public data and checkpoints, the scored readout ranks outcomes more accurately in 12 of 13 cells (two-sided sign test, p approximately 0.003), by 1.5 to 14.5 points in area under the receiver operating characteristic curve (AUC). Paired bootstrap confidence intervals exclude zero in every newly measured cell. The gap varies with task-specific supervision and mismatch between training and serving formats, ranging from -2.2 points for an untuned base model to +13.7 for rationale-format supervision. Analysis of approximately 9,000 rationales identifies two correlates: reduced reliance on the dominant predictive feature and convergence on stock formulations. Probability saturation does not track the gap. A third readout, eliciting a probability before any verdict, improves calibration (Brier score from 0.47 to 0.15) while ranking within noise of scoring, but only for outcome rates represented in training; it is worse than scoring when the scored head is already calibrated. We interpret these differences through the objectives matched by each readout, identify training choices that narrow the gap, and propose retaining generated rationales while sourcing ranking from the scored head.

</details>


### [180] [Decision Transformer for UAV-Mounted RIS-Assisted Dynamic D2D Communications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09885)
*Yaxuan Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文研究UAV搭载RIS辅助D2D通信，采用Decision Transformer实现跨场景泛化优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对UAV移动、姿态变化及角度相关RIS反射带来的复杂时变信道，需在移动性、能量与硬件约束下联合优化轨迹、姿态与RIS相位以最大化平均和速率，并解决跨场景泛化问题。

Method: 建模UAV运动与姿态、时变Rician角度及角度相关RIS反射；将UAV轨迹、姿态与RIS相位的联合优化形式化为问题；采用深度强化学习，并基于多场景专家轨迹训练Decision Transformer。

Result: 实验结果显示出有效的跨场景泛化能力：零样本迁移优于直接DRL迁移，在线微调以更少交互达到有竞争力的性能。

Conclusion: Decision Transformer可提升UAV-RIS-D2D通信优化的跨场景泛化与样本效率，是比传统DRL迁移更有效的方案。

Abstract: This paper studies unmanned aerial vehicle (UAV)-mouted reconfigurable intelligent surface (RIS)-assisted device-to-device (D2D) communication with stochastic link activation. It models UAV motion and attitude, time-varying Rician angles, and angle-dependent RIS reflection. A joint optimization of UAV trajectory, attitude, and RIS phases is formulated to maximize average sum rate under mobility, energy, and hardware constraints. The problem is addressed using deep reinforcement learning and a Decision Transformer trained on expert trajectories from multiple scenarios. Results demonstrate effective cross-scenario generalization, with zero-shot transfer outperforming direct DRL transfer and online fine-tuning achieving competitive performance with fewer interactions.

</details>


### [181] [Grounded Evaluation and Repair for NL-to-PDDL Problem Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09898)
*Joana Rosa,Pedro Santos,Valdemar Oliveira,Romão Silva,L. Miguel Silveira,Bruno Martins*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文研究自然语言到PDDL问题的端到端生成流水线，指出仅用语法有效性或规划成功率会高估生成问题对原任务的忠实度，并通过结构化修复、域一致性检查与参照基准对比来评估忠实性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM将自然语言规划描述翻译为PDDL问题实例时，常用评估标准（语法有效性、规划器成功）可能显著高估对原任务的忠实度：生成的问题可能可解析、可求解，却错误表述了初始状态、目标、对象结构或优化目标。因此需要一个能更细致评估忠实度并支持迭代修复的端到端框架。

Method: 提出端到端NL-to-PDDL流水线，结合LLM生成、PDDL解析/规划/验证检查、域一致性检查器、LLM批评器与迭代修复。细粒度修复反馈由域描述、生成问题、自然语言问题描述及运行诊断构造。采用基于基准PDDL问题描述的参照对比做事后分析，包括重命名不变的结构匹配与语义等价（在域支持可用时）。

Result: 在Planetarium、AutoPlanBench及精选PDDL 2.1问题上，运行成功与基准参照重建存在显著分歧；结构化修复有效，且即便运行成功率提升，PDDL 2.1的参照重建仍然困难。

Conclusion: 评估NL-to-PDDL生成不应仅依赖语法或规划成功，而应结合域一致性、批评反馈与参照基准的结构和语义比对；结构化迭代修复可提升忠实度，但复杂PDDL 2.1场景的参照重建仍是挑战。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have shown promise for translating Natural Language (NL) planning descriptions into PDDL problem instances. However, standard evaluation criteria such as syntactic validity or planner success can substantially overestimate faithfulness to the described task: a generated problem may be parseable and solvable while misrepresenting the intended initial state, goal, object structure, or optimization target. This paper studies an end-to-end NL-to-PDDL pipeline that combines LLM generation, checks in terms of PDDL parsing, planning and validation, a domain-conformance checker, an LLM critic, and iterative repair. Fine-grained repair feedback is constructed from the domain description, the generated problem, the natural language problem description, and operational diagnostics. Reference-based comparisons against curated benchmark PDDL problem descriptions are used for post-hoc benchmark analysis, and these offline checks include renaming-invariant structural matching and semantic equivalence, where domain support is available. Across Planetarium, AutoPlanBench, and curated PDDL~2.1 problems, results show that operational success and benchmark-reference reconstruction can diverge substantially. Results also show that structured repair can be useful, and that PDDL~2.1 remains challenging for reference reconstruction, even when operational success improves.

</details>


### [182] [Time-Frequency Geometric Cross-Attention for Chunked Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09925)
*Shengye Dong,Haochen Niu,Hao Liu,Peiwen Lin,Chuang Wang,Shanmin Pang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出TFGCA模块，用可学习小波变换和融合内积与楔积的交叉注意力修复VLA策略在动作块建模上的频率与正交几何盲点，显著提升分布内外性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代视觉-语言-动作（VLA）策略一次前向预测整段动作块，但动作块在模型内部仅被表示为逐时间步的通用隐藏令牌序列，由线性头解码，这忽视了两种运动结构：一是频率结构，动作块叠加了平滑全局趋势与多尺度精细修正，单一令牌将二者纠缠；二是跨相位几何，不同运动相位（接近、接触、抓取调整、稳定）在表示空间中沿近乎正交的方向展开，却任务相关且沿时间轴共同出现。点积注意力以内在相似度度量对齐，偏好对齐令牌，对近正交关系最不敏感，迫使网络绕道恢复这类关系。

Method: 提出时间-频率几何交叉注意力（TFGCA），一个即插即用模块：用逐维度可学习的平稳小波变换把动作块分解为时频令牌；每个时间令牌通过交叉注意力从时频令牌中检索信息，该注意力以可学习权重融合点积（相似度）与楔积模长（对近正交敏感）。残差零初始化，使初始行为与基座一致，因此可直接加到预训练VLA上联合微调。

Result: 相对同源基座，TFGCA在分布内LIBERO平均提升+1.5，分布外LIBERO-Plus提升+6.3，RoboTwin域随机化下随机化平均提升+28.5，三个真实机器人AgiBot A2任务整体成功率提升+11.67个百分点，且分布外增益更大。

Conclusion: TFGCA通过时频分解与几何敏感的交叉注意力，作为即插即用模块有效修复VLA动作块建模的两大盲点，在仿真与真实机器人任务上均带来显著且分布外更优的性能提升。

Abstract: Modern vision-language-action (VLA) policies predict a whole chunk of actions: one to two seconds of coordinated motion emitted in a single forward pass. Yet an action chunk is essentially a short multivariate trajectory, but inside these models it is a sequence of generic per-timestep hidden tokens decoded by a linear head. This under-serves two motion structures. First, frequency: a chunk superimposes a smooth global trend and fine corrective motion across time scales, and a single token entangles them. Second, cross-phase geometry: motions of different phases (reach, contact, grasp adjustment, settling) unfold along very different, near-orthogonal directions in representation space, yet are tightly related for the task and arise across the time axis. Dot-product attention scores alignment by an inner product, so it favors aligned tokens and is least sensitive near orthogonality, leaving such relationships for the network to recover through a detour.
  We introduce Time-Frequency Geometric Cross-Attention (TFGCA), a drop-in module repairing both blind spots. TFGCA uses a per-dimension learnable stationary wavelet transform to decompose the action chunk into time-frequency tokens, and each time token retrieves information from them via a cross-attention that fuses the dot product (similarity) with the wedge-product magnitude (sensitive to near-orthogonality) through a learnable weight. A zero-initialized residual reproduces the base behavior at initialization, so it can be dropped onto a pretrained VLA and fine-tuned jointly. Relative to the same-source base, TFGCA improves in-distribution LIBERO by +1.5 on average, the OOD LIBERO-Plus by +6.3, the randomized average under RoboTwin domain randomization by +28.5, and the overall success rate on three real-robot AgiBot A2 tasks by +11.67 points, with larger gains out of distribution.

</details>


### [183] [Structural Process Supervision for Latent Chain-of-Thought Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09928)
*Yiqi Li,Xu Chen,Chen Ju,Jiangchao Yao,Zhaoyang Li,Jinsong Lan,Xiaoyong Zhu,Bo Zheng,Yu Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出原型中介过程监督（PMPS），用可学习推理原型为隐式推理嵌入提供结构化的过程级监督，并通过渐进序列对齐（PSA）引导训练，在压缩输出长度的同时提升准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有隐式推理（latent reasoning）方法用紧凑的连续空间嵌入替代冗长的显式思维链（CoT）token，从而提升token效率与鲁棒性，但这些隐式嵌入缺乏直接的过程监督，容易导致表征坍缩与信息分布不均。

Method: 提出PMPS：引入可学习的推理原型作为语义锚点，为隐式推理提供结构化的过程级监督；将隐式嵌入与显式CoT嵌入投影到共享原型空间，通过原型分配实现不等长表示之间的多对多软对齐；同时引入渐进序列对齐（PSA）模块，初始以位置先验鼓励顺序对齐结构，随后逐步放宽以允许自适应匹配。

Result: 在GSM8K-Aug上，PMPS将输出token长度压缩至显式CoT的50%以下；相比领先基线SIM-CoT，在不同模型家族上平均准确率提升2.08%；在GPT-2上甚至超过CoT-SFT；在更大模型和更具挑战性的任务上，在相近输出长度下，PMPS在所有隐式推理方法中取得最高准确率。

Conclusion: PMPS通过原型介导的过程级监督与渐进序列对齐，有效缓解隐式推理的表征坍缩与信息分布不均问题，在显著压缩输出长度的情况下实现准确率的稳定提升，验证了为隐式推理引入结构化过程监督的有效性。

Abstract: Latent reasoning approaches enhance token-level efficiency and robustness by replacing verbose, explicit chain-of-thought (CoT) tokens with compact continuous-space embeddings. However, existing methods lack direct process supervision over these latent embeddings, which often leads to representation collapse and uneven information distribution. To address this, we propose Prototype-Mediated Process Supervision (PMPS), which introduces learnable reasoning prototypes as semantic anchors to provide structural process-level supervision for latent reasoning. PMPS projects latent embeddings and explicit CoT embeddings into a shared prototype space, achieving many-to-many soft alignment between unequal-length representations through prototype assignment. Meanwhile, we introduce a Progressive Sequential Alignment (PSA) module to further guide training: positional priors initially encourage sequential alignment structure, then gradually relax to permit adaptive matching. Experimental results show that PMPS compresses output token length to under 50% of explicit CoT on GSM8K-Aug. Compared to leading baseline SIM-CoT, our method achieves average accuracy gains of 2.08% across different model families. On GPT-2, PMPS even surpasses CoT-SFT. On larger models and a more challenging task, PMPS consistently attains the highest accuracy among all latent reasoning methods with comparable output length.

</details>


### [184] [Belief-State Engine: Augmenting LLMs for Principled Planning Under Partial Observability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10036)
*Arnab Chattopadhayay,Debdipta Halder*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文指出LLM智能体在部分可观测环境中失败的结构性原因在于其仅为历史条件策略、缺乏对隐藏状态的显式信念，并提出将贝叶斯信念推断模块（BSE）置于LLM之外、仅向LLM暴露信念后验的架构方案，在Tiger POMDP与攻击图任务上验证其优于六种基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体虽能生成流畅的动作序列，但在环境部分可观测时会以典型方式失败：模糊反馈导致过早承诺，单条信息性观测可能把不确定性收敛到错误假设，随历史增长策略发生漂移。作者将这些症状归因于一个共同的结构性原因——常规部署的LLM智能体是仅以历史为条件的策略，没有对隐藏状态的显式信念。

Method: 提出Belief-State Engine（BSE）：一个置于LLM外部的推断模块，对给定POMDP模型的潜在状态维护贝叶斯后验，并在每个决策步仅将该后验暴露给LLM，而不展示原始的动作—观测日志。作者给出信念一致性内部状态需满足的四公理最小规范，并证明LLM与BSE的组合是在底层POMDP所诱导的信念MDP上的可靠马尔可夫策略，从而继承经典POMDP理论的Bellman最优性保证（前提是LLM从不接触原始历史）。

Result: 在Tiger POMDP与一项红队攻击图任务上进行评估，对比六种基线：反应式LLM、Chain-of-Thought、ReAct、自然语言信念追踪器、QMDP和POMCP。在两个领域上，BSE增强的智能体在任务回报、信念校准和决策一致性方面均有提升。十项针对性消融实验分离了各架构选择的贡献，并确认该效果并非特定于某一种模型。

Conclusion: LLM智能体在部分可观测环境中的失败源于缺乏显式信念表示这一结构性缺陷，可通过外置贝叶斯信念推断模块加以修复；该架构在理论上具备信念MDP上的马尔可夫策略与最优性保证，并在实验中稳定改善回报、校准与一致性。论文随附代码、环境规范、提示模板与随机种子日志。

Abstract: Large language model agents produce fluent action sequences across a wide range of tasks, yet they fail in characteristic ways once the environment becomes partially observable. Ambiguous feedback pushes them into premature commitments. A single informative observation can collapse their uncertainty onto the wrong hypothesis. Policies drift as the history grows. We trace these symptoms to a common structural cause. An LLM agent, as commonly deployed, is a history-conditioned policy with no explicit belief over hidden state.
  We propose an architectural fix. The Belief-State Engine (BSE) is an inference module placed outside the LLM. It maintains a Bayesian posterior over the latent states of a given POMDP (Partially Observable Markov Decision Process) model, and at each decision step it exposes only that posterior to the LLM. The raw action-observation log is not shown. We set out a minimal four-axiom specification of what a belief-consistent internal state must satisfy, and prove that the LLM paired with the BSE is a sound Markov policy on the belief MDP induced by the underlying POMDP. It therefore inherits the Bellman optimality guarantees of classical POMDP theory, provided the LLM is never exposed to the raw history.
  We evaluate the architecture on the Tiger POMDP and a red-team attack-graph task, against six baselines: a reactive LLM, Chain-of-Thought, ReAct, a natural-language belief tracker, QMDP, and POMCP. Across both domains, the BSE-augmented agent improves task return, belief calibration, and decision consistency. Ten targeted ablations isolate the contribution of each architectural choice confirms that the effect is not specific to any one model. Code, environment specifications, prompt templates, and seed logs accompany this paper.

</details>


### [185] [OntologyAligner: Ontology-Aligned Retrieval and Hierarchy-Guided Large Language Model Reranking for Biomedical Ontology Normalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10055)
*Jie Song,Zhichuan Xu,Ziyu Lu,Meng Xiao,Cheng Bi,Yuxin Zhang,Xin Zheng,Xiaoran Li,Qiongfang Cao,Hao Yang,Bairong Shen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出OntologyAligner三阶段框架与PhenoNormBench基准，实现生物医学本体规范化SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生物医学本体规范化需将自由文本映射到标准概念，但词汇变异及层级相关概念间的细微差异使概念边界模糊，任务仍具挑战性。

Method: OntologyAligner三阶段框架：本体对齐检索、大语言模型候选重排序、选择性层级引导精炼；并构建含13,390样本、覆盖七个HPO数据集的PhenoNormBench基准。

Result: HPO规范化达SOTA：Macro Top-1 88.78%、Micro Top-1 86.75%，分别超最强基线4.85与5.07个百分点；消融显示三阶段互补，敏感性分析表明跨候选集规模与模型主干稳定；在MONDO、MEDIC、NCBITaxon上验证可移植性。

Conclusion: OntologyAligner为生物医学文本到结构化本体概念的准确映射提供通用框架，基准与代码已开源。

Abstract: Biomedical ontology normalization maps free-text expressions to standardized concepts, enabling consistent integration and analysis of biomedical data. This task remains challenging because lexical variation and subtle distinctions among hierarchically related concepts can obscure concept boundaries. We present OntologyAligner, a three-stage framework that combines ontology-aligned retrieval, large language model candidate reranking, and selective hierarchy-guided refinement. We also construct PhenoNormBench, a unified benchmark comprising 13,390 samples from seven Human Phenotype Ontology datasets. OntologyAligner achieved state-of-the-art performance on HPO normalization, with 88.78% Macro Top-1 Accuracy and 86.75% Micro Top-1 Accuracy, exceeding the strongest baseline by 4.85 and 5.07 percentage points, respectively. Ablation analyses showed complementary contributions from all three stages, and sensitivity analyses demonstrated stability across candidate-set sizes and model backbones. Applications to MONDO, MEDIC, and NCBITaxon further established portability to other ontologies. OntologyAligner offers a generalizable framework for accurate mapping of biomedical text to structured ontology concepts. PhenoNormBench and the code are publicly available at https://github.com/zhelishisongjie/OntologyAligner.

</details>


### [186] [Reference-Based Bias Detection in LLMs via Relative Representations of Hidden States](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10060)
*Marek Jeliński,Jan Dubiński,Maciej Chrabaszcz,Sebastian Cygert*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出基于参考表示的偏差审计方法，通过锚句相似度对齐隐藏状态，用表示偏差漂移ΔB检测微调后模型的偏差变化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有偏差审计依赖模型输出，需要昂贵基准或评判模型，且可能遗漏未体现在生成文本中的内部表征变化。

Method: 对相关模型变体（如微调前后）的隐藏状态表示进行审计：由于微调重塑表示几何，绝对隐藏状态不可直接比较，因此用句子与固定锚句集合的相似度编码为共享比较空间中的相对表示，再测量目标群体与正负属性关联的漂移量，即表示偏差漂移ΔB。

Result: 在三个模型家族及WildGuardMix、DecodingTrust、ToxiGen基准上，18个设置中有15个ΔB与输出级偏差变化相关，全量微调下|r|=0.84（p<0.001）；阈值化ΔB检测偏差增加的检查点ROC AUC为0.65–0.99，在WildGuardMix和DecodingTrust上优于SEAT基线；对锚句集、属性集和目标模板变化稳定。

Conclusion: 该方法无需任务特定评测数据，约三分钟审计一个模型，计算量比输出级基准少3–50倍，是输出级审计的补充而非替代。

Abstract: Existing bias auditing methods typically rely on model outputs, requiring costly benchmarks or judge models and potentially missing internal shifts that never appear in generated text. We propose a reference-based method that audits bias in hidden-state representations across related model variants, for example before and after fine-tuning. Because fine-tuning reshapes representation geometry, absolute hidden states are not directly comparable, so we encode each sentence by its similarities to a fixed set of anchor sentences, yielding relative representations in a shared comparison space. There we measure how target groups shift in their association with positive and negative attributes, a quantity we call the Representational Bias Shift $ΔB$. Across three model families and the WildGuardMix, DecodingTrust and ToxiGen benchmarks, $ΔB$ correlates with output-level bias change in 15 of the 18 settings we test, reaching $|r| = 0.84$ ($p < 0.001$) under full fine-tuning and becoming more model-dependent under parameter-efficient adaptation. Thresholding $ΔB$ detects checkpoints whose bias increased with ROC AUC between $0.65$ and $0.99$, and on WildGuardMix and DecodingTrust it separates them better than a SEAT-based baseline for all three families. $ΔB$ is also stable under changes to the anchor set, attribute sets and target templates. Our method requires no task-specific evaluation data and audits a model in about three minutes, using $3$-$50\times$ less compute than the output-level benchmarks considered here. We view it as complementary to output-based auditing rather than a replacement for it.

</details>


### [187] [RAP: Research Attention Prediction Reveals Target-Conditioned Evidence Acquisition Biases](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10092)
*Yingqian Wu,Jingcong Liang,Siyuan Wang,Zhenfei Yin,Philip Torr,Junchi Yu,Zhongyu Wei*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出Research Attention Prediction滚动基准，评估LLM作为科研智能体预测研究热点变化的能力，发现预测策略总体不如精确计数的EWMA基线，并定位到近期证据检索不足与未来特定更新有限两个瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM日益被用作科研智能体，但其追踪研究关注度变化的能力难以评估，因为综述与研究想法缺乏可唯一验证的结果。作者希望建立一个可验证、可重复的评估框架来衡量这一能力。

Method: 构建RAP（Research Attention Prediction）滚动基准，覆盖278个AI/ML领域、1390个episode。在每个时间切点，LLM智能体在时间受限的arXiv语料中检索，并预测未来六个月八个固定研究方向上的论文份额。对比四种诊断模型、精确计数EWMA基线，并做累计历史访问、冻结证据重放、微调等实验。

Result: 检索总体有帮助，但四个诊断模型在综合准确率上均不如精确计数的EWMA基线。累计历史访问下，State carry-forward优于直接Forecast；冻结证据重放显示该反转部分源于Forecast导向策略检索到的近期证据比例更小。即使给出精确历史活跃度，未来特定更新仍受限，仅GPT-5.5配合重新开放的Search略超EWMA。在留出领域上，基于真实结果微调使Qwen3-4B的预测Spearman相关提升0.105，在变化剧烈episode上也有增益。

Conclusion: 当前LLM智能体在预测研究关注度转移上尚不及简单统计基线，主要瓶颈是近期证据检索不足与未来特定更新能力有限；基于真实结果微调可带来一定改进，RAP为后续研究提供了可验证的评测平台。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) increasingly act as research agents, yet their ability to track shifts in research attention is difficult to evaluate because reviews and research ideas lack uniquely verifiable outcomes. We introduce Research Attention Prediction (RAP), a rolling benchmark covering 278 AI/ML fields and 1,390 episodes. At each cut-off, an LLM agent searches a temporally restricted arXiv corpus and predicts the next six months' paper shares across eight frozen research directions. Search generally helps, but all four diagnostic models perform worse than an exact-count exponentially weighted moving average (EWMA) baseline in compositional accuracy. We identify two linked bottlenecks. Under cumulative-history access, State carry-forward outperforms direct Forecast for all four diagnostic models; frozen-evidence replay links a shared component of this reversal to Forecast-oriented policies retrieving a smaller share of recent evidence. Even with exact historical activity, future-specific updating remains limited, with only GPT-5.5 plus reopened Search slightly surpassing EWMA. Fine-tuning on realised outcomes improves Qwen3-4B's forecast Spearman correlation by 0.105 on held-out fields at later origins, with gains also on change-rich episodes.

</details>


### [188] [Agent-Based ML-LLM Fusion with Self-Optimizing Prompts for Plateau Weather Alerts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10135)
*Shuai Yan,Yang Xu,Shan He*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 SmartWeatherAgent 三阶段统一架构（意图识别、灾害预测、推理增强生成），融合规则方法与 LLM，并用增强 LightGBM 实现高精度低延迟的气象灾害预警，综合质量得分提升 112%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旅游气象服务存在上下文融合不足、泛化能力弱、场景适应性差的问题，需要更主动、可解释、智能化的气象服务方案。

Method: 构建三阶段统一架构：意图识别、灾害预测、推理增强生成；融合规则方法与 LLM 进行多粒度查询解析；采用融入高原特有特征（如风速突变率）的 LightGBM 模型；并通过 12 轮微步提示词自优化循环迭代生成质量。

Result: 在高风、降水、低温事件上 F1-Macro 达 0.605，延迟 1.60 ms；综合预警质量得分 S_final 从 4.2（B01）提升至 8.9（B12，+112%）；B08 因数据来源引用由 6.5 升至 8.5，B10 依靠物理机制解释保持高位，B12 科学严谨性达 9.2。

Conclusion: 系统可自动生成融合因果机制、时空演变、定量证据、法规引用与置信度声明的结构化预警，提升专业深度、逻辑严谨性与科学性，推动气象服务向主动感知、可解释决策与智能代理发展。

Abstract: To address insufficient contextualization, weak generalization, and poor scenario adaptation in tourism meteorological services, we propose SmartWeatherAgent--a unified three-stage architecture integrating intent recognition, hazard prediction, and reasoning-enhanced generation. The system fuses rule-based methods with large language models to parse queries at multiple granularities and employs a LightGBM model enriched with highland-specific features (e.g., wind speed abruptness rate), achieving an F1-Macro score of 0.605 with 1.60 ms latency on high-wind, precipitation, and low-temperature events. A 12-round micro-step prompt self-optimization loop boosts the composite warning quality score S_final from 4.2 (B01) to 8.9 (B12, +112%). Key improvements include a sharp rise in B08 from data source citation (6.5 -> 8.5), sustained high performance in B10 via physical mechanism explanation, and a peak scientific rigor score of 9.2 in B12 through explicit uncertainty statements. The system autonomously generates structured warnings that integrate causal mechanisms, spatiotemporal evolution, quantitative evidence, regulatory references, and confidence statements--enhancing professional depth, logical rigor, and scientific soundness, and advancing meteorological services toward proactive perception, explainable decision-making, and intelligent agency.

</details>


### [189] [Kernel-Managed Shared Memory for System-Wide Personalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10144)
*Ryan Lum,Yongfeng Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出并评估"内核管理的共享内存"机制，在AIOS上实现，通过系统内核统一管理多智能体间记忆的检索、隐私与注入，在1800次试验中显著提升个性化并降低成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI系统在多智能体场景下更具价值，但一个智能体学到的有用上下文往往无法被其他智能体获取；将检索与隐私管理交给各个智能体本身存在效率与安全缺口。

Method: 在AIOS上实现kernel-managed shared memory：专用智能体写入结构化、带标签的记忆，而由智能体内核（而非单个智能体）统一负责检索、隐私执行与提示注入。与三种对照方案（未受管的外部记忆后端Mem0、标准检索增强注入、完整无过滤上下文拼接）在GPT-4o、Llama-3.1:8B、Qwen-2.5:7B三种模型上进行对比，共1,800次试验。

Result: 相比同一底层存储的Mem0，内核管理检索与注入使个性化得分提升2.4-4.0分（5分制，如GPT-4o上profile usage从1.05升至4.69），所有对比p<10^-18；相比标准RAG注入增益同样大且跨模型一致；相比完整无过滤上下文拼接，在两模型上统计相当、第三模型略有小幅落后，但提示更短，端到端延迟降低15-61%，token用量与推理成本相应下降。

Conclusion: 将记忆管理集中于智能体系统内核（而非交由个体智能体），能以更低成本获得接近无约束上下文的个性化收益，说明系统级抽象在多智能体记忆管理上兼具效果与效率优势。

Abstract: AI systems become more useful when they can adapt to the people using them, but in multi-agent systems, useful context learned by one agent often remains unavailable to others. We present kernel-managed shared memory, a system-level abstraction in which specialized agents write structured, tagged memories while the agent-system kernel, not individual agents, governs retrieval, privacy enforcement, and prompt injection. We implement and evaluate this design on AIOS and compare it against three alternatives across three assistant models (GPT-4o, Llama-3.1:8B, Qwen-2.5:7B) and 1,800 total trials. Against an unmanaged external memory backend (Mem0) using identical underlying storage, kernel-managed retrieval and injection improve personalization scores by 2.4-4.0 points on a 5-point scale (e.g., 1.05 to 4.69 profile usage on GPT-4o), with every comparison significant at p < 10^-18. Against standard retrieval-augmented injection, gains are similarly large and consistent across all three models. Against full, unfiltered context concatenation, a soft ceiling on available context rather than on response quality, kernel-managed injection statistically matches performance on two of three models and shows a small, model-specific deficit on the third, while using substantially shorter prompts: end-to-end latency is 15-61% lower across all three models, with corresponding reductions in per-call token usage and inference cost. These results indicate that centralizing memory management in the agent-system kernel, rather than leaving retrieval and privacy enforcement to individual agents, delivers most of the personalization benefit of unconstrained context at a fraction of its cost.

</details>


### [190] [Beyond Surface Imitation: Contrastive Modeling for Reasoning Path Alignment in Multimodal In-Context Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10177)
*Mingbo Yang,Wenqiang Wang,Zhaolu Kang,Peng Chen,Yannan Chen,Sunshang Wang,Yan Xiao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种结合对比式示例建模与MLLM自我精炼能力的多模态上下文学习框架，并引入响应条件检索与轻量对齐控制器，在包括VQA在内的三类多模态任务上取得稳定提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态ICL方法多依赖对示例的表面模仿，难以让MLLM对齐给定多模态输入所需的推理路径，在复杂任务中尤为明显，限制了性能进一步提升。

Method: 框架将每个示例重构为：在相同输入下显式对比一个次优响应与一个更优响应，并给出揭示应如何精炼响应的推理路径；同时提出响应条件检索机制，依据当前响应选择推理路径更相关的示例；并使用轻量对齐控制器预测响应质量，判断是否需要进一步精炼。

Result: 在三类多模态任务上的实验表明，所提框架能一致提升MLLM性能，在视觉问答（VQA）任务上提升尤为显著。

Conclusion: 通过对比式示例建模与响应条件检索、对齐控制相结合，可引导MLLM超越表面模仿、沿显式推理路径精炼响应，从而提升多模态任务表现。

Abstract: In-context learning (ICL) is widely used in multimodal large language models (MLLMs) and achieves strong performance across a wide range of multimodal tasks. However, existing multimodal ICL methods often rely on surface level imitation of in-context demonstrations, making it difficult for MLLMs to align their responses with the reasoning path required by the given multimodal input. This limitation becomes more pronounced in complex multimodal tasks, thereby restricting further improvements in MLLM performance. To address this issue, we propose a new multimodal ICL framework that combines contrastive demonstration modeling with the self-refinement capability of MLLMs. Specifically, our framework reformulates each demonstration by explicitly contrasting a suboptimal response with a better response under the same input, together with a reasoning path that reveals how the response should be refined. This contrastive formulation makes the reasoning path toward the desired response more explicit and guides the MLLM beyond superficial imitation. Furthermore, because effective refinement depends on the current response, we introduce a response-conditioned retrieval mechanism to select demonstrations whose reasoning paths are more relevant to the current response. In addition, we use a lightweight alignment controller to predict response quality and determine whether further refinement is needed. Experiments on three types of multimodal tasks show that the proposed framework consistently improves MLLM performance, with particularly notable gains on visual question answering (VQA).

</details>


### [191] [Why Sample What You Can Enumerate? Exact Policy Optimization for Genomic Tool Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10221)
*Haoyue Liu,Xiaoyu Ma,Ye Chen,Zhichao Wang,Xiaoying Tang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文指出在工具子集空间可枚举的专业科学场景中，GRPO 因采样rollout有限而产生结构性不匹配，且随着训练收敛优势信号消失，遂提出 FGPO，通过枚举全部工具子集并预计算奖励表实现精确策略优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在专业科学领域，可用工具组合虽呈组合爆炸但数量有限、可完全枚举，且少量重复出现的计算能力即可覆盖整个领域。此时沿用"冻结推理器+GRPO"的常规配方会出现结构性错配：GRPO 仅用少量采样 rollout 估计动作期望，且随着策略逐渐集中于偏好子集，采样奖励发生碰撞，组归一化优势趋于消失。作者以基因组推理为例，指出无奖励信号的问题比例从均匀参考策略下的 0.2% 升至 GRPO 训练后的 20.8%，说明该错配在训练成功时反而加剧。

Method: 作者提出 FGPO（Full-Group Policy Optimization）作为补救方案，包含两个关键设计：(1) 对每一个工具子集进行打分，优化精确的动作期望，使每次更新都能看到完整的动作空间，而非仅依赖少量 rollout 的估计；(2) 将每个"问题—子集"对的奖励预先计算成一张穷举表，从而把冻结推理器的调用完全移出训练循环。

Result: 在五个冻结推理器和三个基因组基准上，FGPO 在全部 15 个设置中均优于 GRPO，平均提升 6.75 分，最高提升 14.20 分。相比之下，标准的按需 GRPO 调度需要 2.4 倍于 FGPO 的冻结推理器奖励评估次数；在 GenomeQA 上，FGPO 将每题调用的工具数从 2.36 降至 1.40。

Conclusion: 在工具子集可枚举的专业科学场景下，GRPO 的采样式优势估计会随训练收敛而失效，而 FGPO 通过枚举全动作空间与预计算奖励表，既提升了性能又大幅降低了推理器调用成本与工具使用数量，是该类场景下更合适的策略优化方法。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning over a frozen reasoner has become a common recipe for teaching a policy which external tools to invoke. We show that this recipe becomes structurally mismatched in specialist scientific settings where the complete tool-subset space is enumerable. There, a small set of recurring computational capabilities covers the domain, so the space of tool subsets is combinatorial yet small enough to enumerate, and GRPO still estimates an action expectation from a handful of sampled rollouts. Worse, the approximation degrades as training succeeds: as the policy concentrates on preferred subsets it resamples them, sampled rewards collide, and the group-normalized advantage vanishes. On genomic reasoning the fraction of questions yielding no reward signal rises from 0.2% under a uniform reference policy to 20.8% after GRPO training. As a remedy, we introduce FGPO (Full-Group Policy Optimization), which (1) scores every tool subset and optimizes the exact action expectation, so each update sees the complete action space, and (2) precomputes the reward of each question--subset pair into an exhaustive table, removing frozen-reasoner calls from the training loop entirely. Across five frozen reasoners and three genomic benchmarks, FGPO outperforms GRPO in all 15 settings by 6.75 points on average and up to 14.20, while a standard on-demand GRPO schedule would require 2.4 times as many frozen-reasoner reward evaluations and, on GenomeQA, FGPO cuts invoked tools per question from 2.36 to 1.40.

</details>


### [192] [What Should an Agent Forget? Separating What Is Stored from What Is Used](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10263)
*Yuhang Li,Yuchen Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出免训练的RD-Forget框架，将"存储什么"与"使用什么"分离，通过保留源档案并构建查询条件化记忆视图，在压缩预算下兼顾当前状态答案与历史查询需求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 持久化语言智能体需要长期保留经验，但每条回答又只需与特定问题相关的证据。已被更新（取代）的事实若不加控制地参与推理，会误导当前状态回答，同时它对历史性查询仍然必要。因此需要一种机制，既保留完整历史，又能按查询意图选择性控制旧信息的影响。

Method: RD-Forget 是一个免训练框架，核心是分离存储与使用：(1) 保留源档案保存原始观察；(2) 查询条件化记忆视图决定这些信息对当前答案的影响。使用冻结语言模型作为策展器，抽取相关证据、将事实归入语义槽，并保留多跳推理所需的关系；通过同槽替换链接在当前语境下抑制被取代的取值，同时借助意图感知检索让早期证据在历史性查询中重新可用；以率失真（rate-distortion）公式指导在记忆预算内构造回答时视图。

Result: 实验覆盖对话记忆、知识更新、事实整合、长上下文推理和个性化五类任务，采用统一回答流程。结果表明准确回答同时依赖查询相关证据的构建与对过时备选项的控制：缺乏遗忘或缺乏查询条件化的配置得分下降最大；语义槽分组、历史访问与关系保留则提供互补的功能增益。

Conclusion: 在保留历史的同时有选择地控制其使用，是一种兼顾事实变化与未来问题的实用方案，能在有限记忆预算下维持持久语言智能体的可靠性。

Abstract: Persistent language agents need stored experience to remain available across time, while each answer requires evidence suited to a particular question. A superseded fact can mislead a current-state answer and still be essential for a historical query. We present RD-Forget, a training-free framework that separates what an agent stores from what it uses. A retained source archive preserves observations, and a query-conditioned memory view controls their influence on the current answer. A frozen language-model curator extracts relevant evidence, groups facts into semantic slots, and preserves the relations needed for multi-hop reasoning. Same-slot replacement links suppress superseded values in current-state contexts, while intent-aware retrieval makes earlier evidence eligible again. A rate-distortion formulation guides construction of the answer-time view within a memory budget. Experiments span conversational memory, knowledge updating, fact consolidation, long-context reasoning, and personalization under a shared answering pipeline. The results associate accurate answers with both query-relevant evidence construction and control over obsolete alternatives. Configurations without forgetting or query conditioning have the largest score deficits, while slot grouping, historical access, and relation preservation contribute complementary functions. Retaining history while selectively controlling its use offers a practical way to accommodate changing facts and future questions.

</details>


### [193] [TRACE: Training Reasoning Agents for Causal Exploration with Synthesized Rewards](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10315)
*Rui Sun,Zhan Shi,Bing He*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出将可验证奖励强化学习（RLVR）扩展到缺乏廉价真值标签的诊断推理任务中，通过控制模拟器采样隐藏干预并自动生成带噪观测与客观奖励。作者构建了数字广告诊断环境TRACE（12种根因、细粒度细分归因），在235条测试集上，先通过监督微调将Qwen3.5-35B-A3B从0.159提升到0.637，再经合成奖励的强化学习提升至0.757，超越所有评测的提示基线（包括Claude Opus 5与Qwen3.5-122B-A10B），且工具调用次数显著减少。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RLVR已在数学、代码等答案易于验证的领域推动语言模型推理，但复杂数据上的诊断推理缺乏这一优势：确定异常的真实原因通常需要昂贵的专家调查，事后仍可能模糊不清。论文因此追问：这种"验证的不对称性"能否被工程化地构造出来，从而让原本难以获得客观奖励的诊断任务也能进行可扩展的强化学习。

Method: 核心思路是"基于模拟的验证"：采样一个干预，将其注入受控模拟器，并生成该干预会产生的观测。隐藏的干预提供oracle标签与客观奖励，而智能体仍需在噪声、混杂与分布式证据中开展调查。该方法被具体化为TRACE——一个数字广告诊断环境，包含12种根因与细粒度细分归因。智能体在每个episode中使用Python和SQL进行调查，必须识别根因，并在适用时给出受影响的细分归因。

Result: 在235条episode的留出测试集上，最强的提示基线Claude Opus 5达到0.686 FullAttr@1。监督微调将Qwen3.5-35B-A3B从0.159提升到0.637，随后使用合成奖励进行强化学习达到0.757，超过所有评测的提示基线，包括前沿闭源模型以及提示式Qwen3.5-122B-A10B。此外，所得策略使用的工具调用次数也明显少于提示式35B基座模型。

Conclusion: 结果表明，能否获得可扩展、客观的训练信号，可能比单纯的模型规模更为关键。更广泛地看，基于模拟的验证能够让原本模糊的诊断推理任务适配可扩展的强化学习，为在缺乏廉价真值验证的领域应用RLVR提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) has advanced language-model reasoning in domains such as mathematics and code, where objective answers are inexpensive to check. Diagnostic reasoning over complex data lacks this advantage: establishing the true cause of an anomaly often requires costly expert investigation and may remain ambiguous after the fact. We ask whether this asymmetry of verification can instead be engineered. We sample an intervention, inject it into a controlled simulator, and generate the observations it would produce. The hidden intervention provides an oracle label and objective reward, while the agent must still investigate noisy, confounded, and distributed evidence.
  We instantiate this approach in TRACE, a digital-advertising diagnostic environment with 12 root causes and fine-grained segment attribution. Agents investigate each episode using Python and SQL and must identify both the root cause and, when applicable, the affected segment assignment. On a held-out 235-episode test set, the strongest prompted baseline, Claude Opus 5, reaches 0.686 FullAttr@1. Supervised fine-tuning raises Qwen3.5-35B-A3B from 0.159 to 0.637, and subsequent RL with synthesized rewards reaches 0.757, outperforming all evaluated prompted baselines, including frontier closed-source models and a prompted Qwen3.5-122B-A10B model. The resulting policy also uses substantially fewer tool calls than the prompted 35B base. These results provide evidence that access to a scalable, objective training signal can be a more important constraint than model scale alone. More broadly, simulation-based verification can make otherwise ambiguous diagnostic reasoning tasks amenable to scalable reinforcement learning.

</details>


### [194] [From Symbolic Perception to Logical Deduction: A Framework for Guiding Language Models in Geometric Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10335)
*Weichen Dai,Rafael Medeiros Cabral,Ziyi Shou,Yan Cao,Xin Shen,Dongcai Lu,Yi Zhou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 纯大语言模型（LLM）结合几何视觉解析器与符号求解器，在复杂平面几何问题上可比肩先进多模态大模型，并提供了新基准与可解释推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 平面几何对AI仍是重大挑战，需要视觉感知与数学推理的结合。大型多模态模型虽能处理视觉-语言输入，但计算开销大、推理过程不透明、易产生幻觉。作者希望探索是否能用更轻量、更可解释的纯LLM方案来达到同等甚至更好的几何推理效果。

Method: 提出一个框架，将纯LLM与专门模块结合：一是几何视觉解析器，将几何图形转化为符号化表示；二是符号求解器，执行形式化演绎推理。通过这种符号化的方式减少幻觉并提升推理的可解释性。同时构建了一个来自2025年中国中考的难题基准，以保证数据新颖性并考察更深的演绎能力。

Result: 实验表明，该方法的性能与Gemini 2.5 Pro相当，并且能够给出更清晰、更接近人类解题风格的解答。

Conclusion: 纯LLM在配备符号化视觉解析与形式化求解模块后，能够在不依赖大型多模态模型的情况下有效解决复杂平面几何问题，兼顾性能、可解释性与幻觉抑制，为几何AI提供了一条可行且更透明的路径。

Abstract: Plane geometry remains a significant challenge in AI, requiring the integration of visual perception and mathematical reasoning. While Large Multimodal Models (LMMs) naturally handle visuo-linguistic inputs, they are often computationally intensive and opaque. We demonstrate that a pure Large Language Model (LLM), when equipped with specialized modules, can rival state-of-the-art LMMs on complex geometry problems. Our framework integrates a Geometric Vision Parser, which translates diagrams into symbolic form, with a Symbolic Solver that performs formal deductions, thereby mitigating hallucinations and promoting interpretable reasoning. To enable rigorous evaluation, we curate a benchmark of challenging problems from the 2025 Chinese Zhongkao examinations, ensuring data novelty and testing deeper deductive skills. Experiments demonstrate that our approach achieves performance comparable to Gemini 2.5 Pro while delivering clearer, human-like solutions.

</details>


### [195] [Cyber-Financial Contagion: Modeling the Propagation of an AI Vendor Compromise Through the Banking System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10350)
*Alex Leytes*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文研究银行体系共享AI供应商的集中性风险，构建四层异质网络与CFC-Prop传播模型，并训练CFC-GNN早期预警模型，证明AI供应商网络集中是系统性金融稳定问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 银行体系在欺诈筛查、信贷决策、反洗钱分诊、客户分析和内部决策支持等方面高度依赖少数共享AI供应商。一旦某供应商被攻陷，可能沿运营、信息与财务链传播，引发类似传统银行危机的损失。作者希望量化这一被忽视的系统性风险，并为监管者提供可操作工具。

Method: 构建耦合AI供应商、金融机构、同业敞口和客户账户的四层异质网络；提出CFC-Prop随机流行病-清算模型在该网络上模拟风险传播；并训练CFC-GNN早期预警模型，利用供应商侧事件遥测和网络结构识别高连锁风险供应商。实验使用60个供应商、220家银行、约2500条供应商-银行服务边和1400条同业敞口的合成数据集。

Result: CFC-Prop复现了重尾损失分布以及对补丁延迟的强依赖，与前人网络金融证据一致。CFC-GNN在四个基线上达到AUROC 0.82、AUPRC 0.60，并保持校准误差有界。论文发布完整代码、合成数据和可复现脚本。

Conclusion: AI供应商的网络安全集中是一阶金融稳定问题，需纳入监管视野。所提模型为监管者提供了量化推理工具，有助于在冲击发生前识别高风险供应商并评估连锁损失。

Abstract: The banking system now depends on a small set of shared artificial intelligence vendors for fraud screening, credit decisioning, anti-money-laundering triage, customer analytics, and internal decision support. This paper studies how a compromise inside one of those vendors can propagate along a chain of operational, informational, and financial linkages until it triggers losses that look, from the outside, like a classical banking crisis. We build a four-layer heterogeneous network that couples AI vendors, financial institutions, interbank exposures, and customer accounts, and we propose CFC-Prop, a stochastic epidemic-and-clearing model that runs on that network. On a synthetic dataset with 60 vendors, 220 banks, roughly 2,500 vendor-bank service edges, and 1,400 interbank exposures, CFC-Prop reproduces the heavy-tailed loss distributions and the sharp dependence on patch latency that are consistent with prior cyber-financial evidence. We also train an early-warning model, CFC-GNN, that uses vendor-side incident telemetry and graph structure to flag high-cascade-risk vendors before impact. Across four baselines the proposed model reaches AUROC 0.82 and AUPRC 0.60 while keeping calibration errors bounded. We release the full code, synthetic data, and reproducible scripts. The results argue that cyber concentration among AI vendors is a first-order financial-stability problem and give supervisors a concrete quantitative tool for reasoning about it.

</details>


### [196] [Fortunate Recall: Ontology-Driven Memory Lifecycle Management for Persistent Coherence in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10413)
*Ansuman Mullick,Eray Tüzün*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出Fortunate Recall（FR），一种组合式策略层，通过对个人事实进行行为本体分类并应用类别特定的生命周期策略（时间衰减、槽位替代、事件时有效性、类别感知检索路由），解决LLM记忆系统无界增长与检索精度下降的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLM记忆系统对所有个人事实一视同仁，导致存储无界增长、检索精度下降；核心挑战在于生命周期管理——哪些记忆应持久、哪些应被替换、以何种速率，且需依据每条事实的行为类型来决定。

Method: FR将个人事实分类为10+1行为本体，作为LLM抽取元数据上的确定性函数，实施类别特定的生命周期策略。其基础设施无关实现FR-Bank结合差分时间衰减、槽位键替代、事件时有效性与类别感知检索路由。同时构建LifecycleBench（516题时间消歧基准）用于评估。

Result: FR-Bank在LifecycleBench达76.9%通过率，领先Mem0、A-MEM、Memory-R1、MemoryOS（61%-70.5%）；在LongMemEval-S达75.2%，表明生命周期策略无额外检索成本。预注册消融显示：以三个通用生命周期原语替换类型化层后正确率无显著变化（-1.7pp），说明正确性优势源于通用生命周期元数据；行为本体则带来校准收益，使下游虚构减半（12.0% vs 24.2%，p<0.001）。端到端FR-Bank将虚构从Mem0的45.1%降至22.4%（已答查询）及32.2%降至13.0%（全部查询），同时正确回答更多（31.2% vs 18.6%），排名在开源Kimi K2.5上复现。在BEAM基准上46.8%正确率 vs Mem0的32.9%，本体收益集中于矛盾消解，在约七个策略簇处饱和。

Conclusion: 生命周期策略对标准检索无成本损耗；正确性主要来自通用生命周期元数据，而行为本体主要贡献校准并显著减少虚构；该分解可迁移至独立基准BEAM。本体、基准与代码已开源。

Abstract: Current LLM memory systems treat all personal facts identically, so stores grow without bound while retrieval precision degrades. The core challenge is lifecycle management: which memories should persist, which should be replaced, and at what rate, conditioned on the behavioral type of each fact. Fortunate Recall (FR) is a composable policy layer that classifies personal facts into a 10+1 behavioral ontology and applies category-specific lifecycle policies (differential temporal decay, slot-key supersession, event-time validity, and category-aware retrieval routing) as deterministic functions over LLM-extracted metadata. FR-Bank, our infrastructure-independent implementation, reaches a 76.9% pass rate on LifecycleBench, a new 516-question temporal-disambiguation benchmark, ahead of Mem0, A-MEM, Memory-R1, and MemoryOS (61% to 70.5%), and 75.2% on the full LongMemEval-S under the canonical Wu et al. judge protocol, so lifecycle policies impose no measurable cost on standard retrieval. A pre-registered ablation locates the gains: replacing the typed layer with three generic lifecycle primitives leaves correctness statistically unchanged (-1.7pp, 95% CI [-6.0, +2.7]), so the generic lifecycle metadata carries the correctness advantage, while the behavioral ontology carries calibration, halving downstream confabulation (12.0% vs 24.2%, p<0.001). End-to-end, FR-Bank cuts confabulation from Mem0's 45.1% to 22.4% over answered queries and from 32.2% to 13.0% over all queries while answering more of them correctly (31.2% vs 18.6%); the ranking replicates on the open-weight Kimi K2.5. The decomposition transfers to BEAM, an independently built benchmark: 46.8% correct vs Mem0's 32.9% over 280 questions, with the ontology's benefit concentrated in contradiction resolution and saturating near seven policy clusters. The ontology, benchmark, and code are released.

</details>


### [197] [ConvMem: Convolutional Memory for Long-Context Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10441)
*Hongming Zhang,Zhaozhen Gu,Fengshuo Bai,Ming Hao,Qingyang Zhang,Yuanyuan Wang,Shiyang Tang,Yanna Wang,Bo Xu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ConvMem提出一种免训练、可高度并行化的长上下文推理框架，将长上下文推理重构为层次化卷积，通过分层摘要将推理路径从线性链变为对数树，缓解延迟与过拟合问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM因固定上下文长度限制难以处理超长上下文。现有顺序方法（如MemAgent）通过分段读取并迭代更新固定大小记忆来扩展有效上下文，但存在延迟高、需昂贵RL训练、易在特定数据集上过拟合等问题。

Method: ConvMem将带特定查询提示的LLM视为卷积核，对文本段进行层次化摘要。框架包含可配置步长（Configurable Strides）与跳跃连接（Skip Connections）以增强证据捕获与传播，并采用多核卷积（Multi-Kernel Convolution）将复杂查询分解为解耦的语义通道，实现文本段与推理线程的大规模并行化。

Result: 在RULER-HotpotQA和RULER-2WikiMultiHopQA上，ConvMem优于免训练基线，并避免了RL训练模型在分布外任务上常见的参数化先验过拟合风险。

Conclusion: ConvMem是一种免训练、高度可并行的长上下文推理框架，显著缩短推理路径并降低错误累积，相比顺序RL方法更具泛化性。

Abstract: While Large Language Models (LLMs) have demonstrated impressive capabilities, they often struggle with extremely long contexts due to fixed context limits. To address this, sequential approaches like MemAgent extend the effective context by reading text in segments and iteratively updating a fixed-size memory. However, this sequential paradigm suffers from high latency and requires costly reinforcement learning (RL) training, which can lead to overfitting on specific datasets. To overcome these limitations, we propose ConvMem, a training-free, highly parallelizable framework that reformulates long-context reasoning as a hierarchical convolution. Inspired by CNNs, ConvMem treats an LLM prompted with a specific query as a convolutional kernel. This kernel summarizes text segments hierarchically, shortening the reasoning path from a linear chain into a logarithmic tree. Specifically, ConvMem integrates \textit{Configurable Strides} and \textit{Skip Connections} to ensure robust evidence capture and propagation, while employing \textit{Multi-Kernel Convolution} to decompose complex queries into disentangled semantic channels. This design not only mitigates error accumulation but also enables massive parallelization across both text segments and reasoning threads. Experiments on RULER-HotpotQA and RULER-2WikiMultiHopQA demonstrate that ConvMem outperforms training-free baselines and avoids the risk of overfitting to parametric priors often observed in RL-trained models on out-of-distribution tasks.

</details>


### [198] [JarvisGUI: Towards Cross-Device GUI Agents with Dynamic Task Composition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10451)
*Zixiang Chen,Yuheng Lu,Zihao Cheng,Zeming Liu,Jizeng Bai,Ziye Huang,Zhiyin Lin,Zihan Li,Yuhang Guo,Yunhong Wang,Haifeng Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 JarvisGUI，一个跨设备、跨平台（Android/Windows/Ubuntu）的动态 GUI agent 基准，通过轻量类型系统将任务建模为输入-输出变换，自动组合多步跨设备工作流，揭示现有开源 GUI agent 在状态传递、跨平台推理与长程依赖管理上的关键能力缺口。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实中的 GUI 使用常跨越多个设备和平台，需要传递中间结果、维护共享状态并协调异构环境；但现有 GUI 基准几乎只评测单设备、静态定义的任务，导致对 agent 真实可用性的评估过于乐观，跨设备能力基本未被考察。

Method: JarvisGUI 将 GUI 任务形式化为轻量类型系统下的输入-输出变换，从而支持自动组合多步、跨设备的流程，并在统一框架内对 agent 表现进行动态评测；评测在涵盖多个操作系统的虚拟环境中进行。

Result: 评测显示，当前最先进的开源 GUI agent 在状态传递感知（state-transfer awareness）、跨平台上下文推理以及长程依赖管理方面表现不佳，暴露出一个在现有基准中不可见的关键能力缺口。

Conclusion: 跨设备工作流是评估 GUI agent 真实能力的重要维度；JarvisGUI 通过动态、类型化的跨平台任务组合，弥补了现有单设备静态基准的不足，并指出未来 GUI agent 需重点提升跨设备状态协调与长程依赖管理能力。

Abstract: Real-world GUI usage frequently involves workflows that span multiple devices and platforms, requiring the transfer of intermediate results, maintenance of shared state, and coordination across heterogeneous environments. However, existing GUI benchmarks overwhelmingly evaluate agents on single-device, statically defined tasks, thus leaving such cross-device capabilities largely unexamined, resulting in an overly optimistic assessment of agents' readiness for real-world usage. We introduce JarvisGUI, a dynamic benchmark that evaluates GUI agents on cross-device workflows requiring coordinated interaction across heterogeneous platforms, including Android, Windows, and Ubuntu. Specifically, JarvisGUI formulates GUI tasks as input-output transformations under a lightweight type system, which allows us to automatically compose multi-step, cross-device workflows and dynamically evaluate agent performance within a unified framework. By evaluating agents in virtual environments spanning multiple operating systems, JarvisGUI reveals that state-of-the-art open-source GUI agents struggle with the state-transfer awareness, cross-platform contextual reasoning, and long-horizon dependency management required for real-world workflows, exposing a critical capability gap invisible to existing benchmarks.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [199] [Spectral origin of the topological gap exponent d + η: mechanism, kernel, decomposition, and scope](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09159)
*Matthew Loftus*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文解析推导了拓扑间隙 Δ 的标度律 Δ~L^{d+η}，并指出机制要求 d<2+η（物理系统仅限 d=2）；d=3 时谱积分为 UV 主导，需密度归一化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 拓扑间隙 Δ（临界点云相对密度匹配零模型的 H1 超额总持续性）此前仅有经验标度关系 Δ~L^{d+η}，缺乏解析推导与机制解释；同时需要澄清为何 d=3 时需密度归一化、以及 Δ 与谱积分间关联的本质。

Method: 将 Δ 与谱积分 I(α)=Σ_{k≠0} S_conn(k)|k|^α 联系，分析其 IR/UV 主导性：IR 主导时 I(α)~L^{2-α-η}，故 I(-2η)~L^{d+η}；做体积-形状分解 I(-2η)=I0·I_shape。对 L=32–256 的 Potts q=4 做 α-扫描。用 Divol–Polonik 启发式推广至非均匀泊松强度以考察 PH 核的 k 依赖性，并做偏相关分析检验磁化关联。

Result: 推导得 Δ~L^{d+η} 并证明机制仅当 d<2+η（IR 主导）成立，物理系统限于 d=2；d=3 为 UV 主导，解释密度归一化的必要性。α-扫描给出 α_opt∈[-0.75,-0.5]，与 -2η_Ising 一致、与 -2η_{q=4}=-1 不符。⟨m²·I(-2η)⟩ 超标度积由 r(m²,I)≈-0.98 的相关主导，故需协方差校正。PH 裸核平坦，仅在临界时有效核获得 k 依赖。逐构型 Δ 与 I(-0.5) 的 R²=0.91（L=256）在剔除 |M| 后降至约 0。

Conclusion: 逐构型证据支持 I0 的 Parseval 恒等式，但不支持 |k|^{-2η} 形状因子；形状因子由系综 L-标度确立。α_opt 结果暂定，需 L≥1024 验证。

Abstract: The topological gap $Δ$ -- the excess $H_1$ total persistence of a critical point cloud over a density-matched null -- scales as $Δ\sim L^{d+η}$. We derive this analytically: the spectral integral $I(α) = \sum_{k\neq 0} S_{\mathrm{conn}}(k)\,|k|^α$ scales as $L^{2-α-η}$ when IR-dominated, giving $I(-2η) \sim L^{d+η}$. The decomposition $I(-2η) = I_0 \cdot I_{\mathrm{shape}}$ separates volume ($I_0 \propto N(1-m^2) \sim L^d$) from anomalous dimension ($I_{\mathrm{shape}} \sim L^η$); the volume factor accounts for the magnetization-driven per-configuration variance of $Δ$. We prove the mechanism requires $d < 2 + η$ (IR dominance), confining it to $d = 2$ for physical systems; in $d = 3$ the spectral integral is UV-dominated, explaining why density normalization is needed. An $α$-sweep for Potts $q = 4$ at $L = 32$--$256$ finds $α_{\mathrm{opt}}$ in $[-0.75, -0.5]$, consistent with $-2η_{\mathrm{Ising}}$ and inconsistent with $-2η_{q=4} = -1$; we flag this as tentative pending $L \geq 1024$ confirmation. The $\langle m^2 \cdot I(-2η)\rangle$ hyperscaling product is dominated by the correlation $r(m^2, I) \approx -0.98$ via the shared $I_0$ amplitude, so we report it as a covariance-correction analysis. Under a heuristic argument extending Divol--Polonik to inhomogeneous Poisson intensities, the bare PH kernel is flat; the effective kernel acquires $k$-dependence only at criticality. The per-configuration agreement between $Δ$ and $I(-0.5)$ is primarily a magnetization correlation: $R^2 = 0.91$ at $L = 256$ collapses to $R^2 \approx 0$ once $|M|$ is partialed out. Per-configuration evidence corroborates the $I_0$ Parseval identity but not the $|k|^{-2η}$ shape factor; the latter is established by ensemble $L$-scaling.

</details>


### [200] [Physics-informed neural networks by Gradient-Guided Gaussian Adaptive Sampling (3GAS-PINNs)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09162)
*Yousen Wang,Wei Zhao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出梯度引导高斯自适应采样物理信息神经网络（3GAS-PINNs），通过结合均匀分布与基于解的梯度的高斯平滑概率分布来自适应地布置配点，从而在保持全局约束的同时聚焦于高梯度区域，显著提升非线性偏微分方程求解精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统物理信息神经网络（PINNs）在求解非线性问题时，常因固定损失权重和均匀配点分布而面临收敛慢、梯度不平衡，以及难以捕捉激波、孤子等局部间歇结构的问题。这些缺陷源于固定权重和均匀配点无法适应训练过程中解场复杂度的演变。

Method: 提出3GAS-PINNs，将均匀概率分布与由解的空间梯度导出的高斯平滑概率分布相结合：均匀分布维持全局约束满足，梯度引导的高斯分布将配点集中于高梯度区域，从而准确捕捉激波和孤子等间歇结构。

Result: 在一维强迫Burgers方程、Korteweg-de Vries（KdV）方程和非线性薛定谔方程三个具有陡峭梯度或强非线性特征的基准问题上进行评估。与基线PINNs相比，3GAS-PINNs有效提升了间歇区域的物理一致性，数值仿真精度最高提升约14倍。

Conclusion: 3GAS-PINNs通过梯度引导的高斯自适应采样机制，克服了传统PINNs在非线性问题中配点固定与梯度不平衡的局限，显著提高了对激波、孤子等间歇结构的捕捉能力和整体数值求解精度。

Abstract: Physics-informed neural networks (PINNs) provide a mesh-free framework for solving partial differential equations, yet their performance in nonlinear problems is often limited by slow convergence, gradient imbalance, and insufficient resolution to capture localized intermittent structures such as shock waves[1]. These issues arise primarily from the use of fixed weights of loss and uniform collocation point distributions, which cannot adapt to the evolving complexity of the solution field during training. To address these challenges, Gradient-Guided Gaussian Adaptive Sampling Physics-Informed Neural Networks (3GAS-PINNs) is proposed in this paper, which combines uniform probability distribution and Gaussian-smoothed probability distribution derived from the spatial gradients of solution, to maintain global constraint satisfaction as well as concentrating collocation points in regions of high gradient. Thus, intermittency structures like shock wave and solitons can be accurately captured. The method is evaluated on three benchmark nonlinear problems, including one-dimensional forced Burgers equation, Korteweg-de Vries (KdV) equation and nonlinear Schrodinger equation, all of which exhibit steep gradients or strong nonlinearity. In comparison with baseline PINNs, 3GAS-PINNs can effectively promote the physical consistency in intermittent regions. The accuracy of the numerical simulation can be improved by a factor of up to 14.

</details>


### [201] [World-Time Compute with Verified Code World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09163)
*James Schwoebel,Ingrida Semenec,Jenia Rousseva,Marcos Ortiz,Collin Overbay,Christopher Klaus,Anderson Edmond,Manish Bhatt,Rome Thorstenson,Jessica Tsai,Martin G. Frasch*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 将领域动力学写成可执行代码模板，可批量生成符号化世界模型，用其精确标注轨迹微调LLM（world-time compute），能显著提升对未见世界的泛化，尤其在模型小、任务弱时收益最大。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM要在某个领域泛化，通常需要大量真实标注样本，而多数领域缺乏这类数据。作者希望找到一种廉价制造训练数据的方式，并解决由此带来的标注可信度问题。

Method: 当领域动力学可写成代码时，用一个模板实例化出大量世界模型——即符号状态上的可执行、可验证程序，每个都是精确标注轨迹的无限来源。用大量此类世界生成的轨迹微调LLM，称之为world-time compute（测试时计算的训练时对应物）。作者通过验证代码保证标签可信，并设置损坏标签控制实验区分标签精确性与任务多样性。在ARC-AGI网格、List Functions、CLRS等真实基准上以逐世界测试时训练检验，并测试跨世界形式（单个适配器训练于128个不相交世界）。

Result: 在合成世界家族上泛化到未训练过的世界：0.5B模型提升+29点，最大模型提升在噪声范围内（趋于饱和）。合成后校验的动力学在20步展开中精确，并在10倍分布外探测上100%正确，而逐步LLM/MLP预测器误差累积并崩溃。损坏标签对照显示增益源于标签精确性而非任务多样性。List Functions上跨世界形式：单适配器达40%对损坏标签对照6%（+34点，CI[29,39]）。增益是饱和规律而非定律：对少步推理及小/弱模型最大，长链、感知类任务与饱和任务减弱，无共享技能时跨任务迁移弱。

Conclusion: 在符号状态下，用可验证代码世界生成精确标注轨迹进行微调，是提升LLM领域泛化的有效杠杆，收益随能力稀缺程度增大而饱和。作者开源了零依赖框架OpenWorld，代码、配方与手稿可由单一仓库重建；像素原生领域仍属学习模型范畴。

Abstract: LLMs generalize across a domain only after seeing many real, labeled examples, which most domains lack. We study a way to manufacture it cheaply. When a domain's dynamics can be written as code, one template instantiates into many world models: executable, verifiable programs over symbolic state, each an inexhaustible source of exactly-labeled trajectories. Fine-tuning an LLM on trajectories through many such worlds, which we call world-time compute, a training-time analogue of test-time compute, lifts generalization to held-out worlds it never trained on (synthesized world families). Gains are largest where capability is scarcest: +29 points at 0.5B; the largest model's lift is within noise, consistent with saturation. Labels can be trusted because the worlds are verified code: synthesized-then-checked dynamics are exact over 20-step rollouts and answer 10x out-of-distribution probes exactly (100%), whereas per-step LLM and MLP predictors compound error and collapse. Unlike domain randomization, each world is independently authored and verified; a corrupted-label control shows label exactness, not task variety, drives the gains. On real benchmarks (ARC-AGI grids, List Functions, CLRS) the same lever holds as per-world test-time training. On List Functions the harder cross-world form holds: one adapter trained on 128 disjoint worlds reaches 40% on held-out worlds versus 6% for a corrupted-label control (+34 points, CI [29, 39]). The gain is a saturating regularity, not a law: largest for few-step reasoning and small/weak models, fading for long chains, perception-induced tasks, and saturated tasks; cross-task transfer is weak without shared skill. Worlds are authored and served by OpenWorld, a zero-dependency framework (companion paper). Scope: symbolic state; pixel-native domains remain territory of learned models. All code, recipes, and this manuscript regenerate from one repository.

</details>


### [202] [Scaling Post-Training Ternarisation to Qwen3-8B Capability Retention, Reproduction, Lossless Packing, and Packed Execution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09240)
*Anirudh Malik,M Sparsh Mehra,Poojith Devan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 将 Qwen3-4B 的超低比特（1.58 位）训练后转换流程扩展到 Qwen3-8B，给出端到端的规模化验证：困惑度比约 1.36x、零样本准确率保留 78.5%，并实现无损打包与直接执行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: "1.58 比特"这类标称标签并不能说明实际部署的表示形式与执行开销，超低比特模型缺乏可复现、可验证的规模化基线，因此需要一次从 4B 到 8B 的端到端放大刻画。

Method: 采用权重-only A16 配置的训练后转换流程：KOTMS 旋转、E2M-ATQ 自适应三值化，以及 GPTQ 风格的误差补偿（算法本身非本文新贡献）。工作重点是规模化刻画，包括外部复现闸门、匹配的 4B/8B 能力分析、跨语料困惑度、有效比特核算、无损格点感知打包与直接打包执行。

Result: 8B 模型三语料困惑度比为 1.361x（WikiText-2 1.318x、C4 1.393x、PTB 1.371x）；n=500 的八个零样本任务平均准确率 64.6% vs FP16 的 72.4%，机会校正保留率 78.5%，绝对损失 7.8 点；匹配的 4B 仅保留 69.6%，8B 领先 8.9 点。打包检查点为 8.24 GiB 且精度无损；直接执行达 15.52 tokens/s、占用 7.35 GiB，但初步打包 GEMV 仍慢于 FP16 cuBLAS。

Conclusion: 本文给出一个经验证的规模化基线：模型增大可提升对激进训练后离散化的鲁棒性，实际序列化问题在所测产物上已解决，直接执行可行；更多随机种子、校准分布与内核优化仍待探索。

Abstract: Ultra-low-bit language models promise reductions in storage and memory traffic, but a nominal "1.58-bit" label does not specify the deployed representation or its execution cost. We study a scale-up of an aggressive post-training conversion pipeline from Qwen3-4B to Qwen3-8B.
  The conversion uses KOTMS rotation, E2M-ATQ adaptive ternarisation, and GPTQ-style error compensation in a weight-only A16 configuration. We do not claim these algorithms as new. Our contribution is the end-to-end scale-up characterisation: an external reproduction gate, matched 4B/8B capability analysis, cross-corpus perplexity, effective-bit accounting, lossless lattice-aware packing, and direct packed execution.
  The 8B model reaches a three-corpus perplexity ratio of 1.361x, with WikiText-2, C4, and PTB ratios of 1.318x, 1.393x, and 1.371x. On eight zero-shot tasks at n = 500, mean accuracy is 64.6% versus 72.4% for FP16, corresponding to 78.5% chance-corrected retention and a 7.8-point absolute cost. The matched 4B run retains 69.6%, yielding an 8.9-point 8B advantage.
  The packed checkpoint is 8.24 GiB and preserves the recorded perplexity to measurement precision. Direct packed execution reaches 15.52 tokens/s in 7.35 GiB, while a preliminary packed GEMV remains slower than FP16 cuBLAS. The result is a validated scale-up baseline: model size improves robustness to aggressive post-training discretisation, actual serialisation is solved for the measured artefact, and direct execution is feasible, while broader seeds, calibration distributions, and kernel optimisation remain open.

</details>


### [203] [Distribution-Consistent Inference for Dynamic Sparse Mixture-of-Experts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09241)
*Dohyeon Kim,Bedionita Soro,Sung Ju Hwang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文针对SMoE大模型推理时动态路由导致的分布偏移问题，提出轻量级的层级分布对齐（LDA）校正方法。LDA利用层级校准统计量将减少路由后的表示对齐至默认配置，在多个SMoE大模型与基准上恢复了大部分损失性能，同时保持稀疏推理效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 主流MoE模型采用固定的top-k专家选择策略，对每个token分配相同的专家预算，即使更少专家已足够。推理时动态top-k路由虽能在不重训练的情况下减少计算，但现有方法常忽略偏离训练时路由配置所引起的分布偏移。作者发现减少激活专家数会持续增大SMoE输出的RMS尺度与方差，造成表示不匹配，进而在专家容量损失之外进一步导致下游性能下降。

Method: 提出层级分布对齐（Layer-wise Distribution Alignment, LDA），一种轻量级的推理时校正方法。它使用逐层的校准统计量（calibration statistics），将减少路由后的表示与默认配置的表示进行对齐，从而修正可校正的分布偏移部分，且无需重新训练、几乎不增加开销。

Result: 在多个SMoE大模型、多种基准与多种路由策略上，LDA均能恢复因减少路由引发的分布偏移所导致的大部分性能损失，同时保持稀疏推理的效率，额外开销可忽略不计。

Conclusion: 减少激活专家带来的性能下降部分源于可校正的分布偏移（RMS尺度与方差增大导致的表示不匹配）。通过层级校准统计进行推理时对齐，LDA能有效缓解该问题，为动态稀疏路由提供了一种高效且无需重训练的补偿方案。

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) architectures have emerged as a powerful paradigm for scaling model capacity while preserving efficient inference in large foundation models. However, most MoE models use a fixed top-$k$ expert selection policy, assigning the same expert budget to every token even when fewer experts may be sufficient. Inference-time dynamic top-$k$ routing can reduce computation without retraining, but existing methods often overlook the distributional shift caused by deviating from the training-time routing configuration. We show that reducing the number of activated experts consistently increases the RMS scale and variance of SMoE outputs, inducing a representation mismatch that contributes to downstream performance degradation in addition to the loss of expert capacity. To address this correctable component, we propose Layer-wise Distribution Alignment (LDA), a lightweight inference-time correction that uses layer-wise calibration statistics to align reduced-routing representations with the default configuration. Across multiple SMoE LLMs, benchmarks, and routing strategies, LDA recovers much of the performance lost induced by the distributional shift under reduced routing while preserving sparse-inference efficiency with negligible overhead.

</details>


### [204] [DiffLUT-Net: Differentiable Training of FPGA LUT Networks with Learnable Connectivity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09254)
*Jiaqi Ye,Xinrui Gong,Jingcun Wang,Olga Kondrateva,Bing Li,Grace Li Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DiffLUT-Net 提出一种 FPGA 原生的神经网络，通过可微松弛联合学习六输入 LUT 的真值表及其输入源选择，训练后离散化并导出为可综合 Verilog，在五个基准上取得良好的精度-资源权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 FPGA 神经网络部署流程多聚焦于加速乘累加运算，或将预训练量化模型转换为查找表，缺乏从零训练、直接面向 FPGA 逻辑结构的网络设计方法。

Method: 提出 DiffLUT-Net：以六输入 LUT 为基本连接单元，利用可微 LUT 函数松弛与硬件源选择机制，联合学习每个 LUT 的 64 个真值表条目及其六个输入端口来源；训练后将真值表与连接离散化，剪除未使用逻辑，直接导出为可综合的 Verilog。

Result: 在五个基准任务上，DiffLUT-Net 实现了有利的精度-资源权衡，验证了联合学习 LUT 功能与稀疏连接的紧凑 FPGA 原生推理的有效性。

Conclusion: 从零训练、联合学习 LUT 功能与稀疏连接的设计思路，可有效实现紧凑的 FPGA 原生推理网络。

Abstract: Field-programmable gate arrays (FPGAs) enable efficient neural-network inference, but most deployment flows either accelerate multiply-accumulate operations or convert pretrained quantized models into lookup tables (LUTs). We present DiffLUT-Net, an FPGA-native network connected by six-input LUTs that are trained from scratch. We jointly learn the 64 truth-table entries of a LUT and the source to each of its six input ports using a differentiable LUT function relaxation and hardware source selection. After training, the truth tables and connections are discretized, unused logic can be pruned, and the network is exported directly as synthesizable Verilog. Across five benchmarks, DiffLUT-Net achieves favorable accuracy-resource trade-offs. These results demonstrate the effectiveness of jointly learning LUT functions and sparse connectivity for compact FPGA-native inference. The code is available at https://github.com/TUDa-HWAI/DiffLUT-Network.

</details>


### [205] [Accountable and uncertainty-aware evaluation of sensor-based AI under distribution shift: devices, subjects, and nearly three years underground](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09257)
*Benny Platte,Rico Thomanek,Christian Roschke,Marc Ritter*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种分阶段、可问责的传感器AI部署评估协议，用分位数与机会基线替代均值判断，并在矿井地磁定位任务中验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传感器AI系统实际部署时的设备、人员、记录时段常与训练条件不同，随机划分的train-test无法揭示这种分布漂移带来的性能退化；同时仅看均值会掩盖表现的极端风险，导致错误的部署决策。

Method: 设计四级累积泛化阶段的评估协议，逐步留出设备、受试者和时间；每阶段用重复训练的分位数对照具有正确类别数的机会基线，并引入"超出当前范围率"以揭示模型向部署中已不存在的类别发生静默误导，同时以5%分位数的显式决策规则代替均值来决定是否上线。在无基础设施依赖的智能手机地磁定位循环分类器上，于两处真实地下矿井验证，并在第二处复现训练阶段方案。

Result: 在训练活动34个月后、训练时未知的设备代际、且由留出测绘员采集的数据上重新评估未改动的模型：其现况条件化精确率的5%分位数为0.39（299次重复训练），为机会水平的16.5倍；在42个可达地点类别的构成上该指标波动±0.08，可达重复运行间差异的数倍。单配置的重复训练表明均值具有误导性：一个双峰配置能以均值检验决定性通过，但其5%分位数低于机会水平一个数量级以上。

Conclusion: 评估应被视为带声明参考水平和量化不确定性的测量：以分位数而非均值、并配合超范围率与显式决策规则，才能识别传感器AI在真实部署漂移下的风险，避免被均值误导。

Abstract: Sensor-based AI systems are rarely operated under the conditions under which they were trained: devices, personnel and recording epochs change, and each change degrades performance in ways a random train-test split cannot reveal. We propose a staged, accountable evaluation protocol that treats the evaluation of a deployed model as a measurement with declared reference levels and a quantified uncertainty. Four cumulative generalisation stages hold out devices, subjects and time. Each stage is judged on quantiles of repeated trainings against chance references with the correct class count, an out-of-present-scope rate exposes silent misdirection towards classes that are no longer present in deployment relative to training, and an explicit decision rule ties roll-out decisions not to means but to 5% quantiles. We demonstrate the protocol on infrastructure-free geomagnetic localisation with smartphone-based recurrent classifiers in two real underground mines, including a replication of the scheme's training stages at the second site. Unchanged models are re-evaluated on data recorded 34 months after the training campaign, on a device generation unknown at training time and with a held-out surveyor. The 5% quantile of their present-conditioned precision there is 0.39 over 299 repeated trainings, 16.5 times the chance level; across the composition of the 42 reachable location classes the figure varies by +/-0.08, several times the spread between repeated runs. Repeated trainings of a single configuration show why means mislead: a bimodal configuration passes a mean-based test decisively while its 5% quantile lies more than an order of magnitude below chance.

</details>


### [206] [Literati: Towards Anytime Optimal Shape Generalized Trees via AO*](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09299)
*Nakul Upadhya,Eldan Cohen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Literati，首个最优Shape Generalized Tree归纳算法，通过AND/OR图与AO*搜索联合优化树结构和形状函数复杂度，在24个数据集上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 决策树可解释且表格数据表现好，但贪心自顶向下归纳易产生次优且复杂的结构。最优决策树方法虽做全局优化，却限制为轴对齐阈值分裂，表达力受限，常需深层复杂树来捕捉非线性特征效应。Shape Generalized Trees将阈值分裂推广为可学习的单变量形状函数以提升表达力、实现更紧凑的树，但现有SGT归纳算法均为贪心，缺乏最优性保证。

Method: 作者提出Literati，首个最优SGT归纳算法。核心是问题的AND/OR图新表述，可联合优化树结构与形状函数复杂度；并设计基于AO*的求解算法，含两项保持最优性的增强：用于OR节点选择的次级启发式，以及用于AND节点探索的轮询策略，以提升anytime性能。

Result: 在24个真实世界数据集上，Literati的训练与测试准确率均高于当前最先进的树模型方法。

Conclusion: 将最优性引入SGT归纳是可行且有效的，AND/OR图与AO*结合可为可解释模型在表达力与紧凑性之间提供更优的权衡。

Abstract: Decision trees are prized for their interpretability and strong performance on tabular data, but popular greedy top-down induction algorithms can yield suboptimal and unnecessarily complex structures. Optimal decision tree methods address this through global optimization, yet remain restricted to axis-aligned threshold splits, which limit the expressivity of each node and often force deep, complex trees to capture non-linear feature effects. Shape Generalized Trees (SGTs) generalize threshold splits to learnable univariate shape functions, improving expressivity and enabling more compact trees. However, existing SGT induction algorithms are greedy and offer no optimality guarantees. In this work, we introduce Literati, the first algorithm for optimal SGT induction. We propose a novel AND/OR graph formulation of the problem that jointly optimizes tree structure and shape function complexity. To solve this AND/OR graph, we develop an AO*-based algorithm with two enhancements that improve anytime performance while preserving optimality: a secondary heuristic for OR-node selection and a round-robin policy for AND-node exploration. Across 24 real-world datasets, Literati achieves higher training and test accuracy than state-of-the-art tree approaches.

</details>


### [207] [Explaining f-Divergence-Based Regularization via Local Curvature and Sharpness-Aware Minimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09367)
*Nour Jamoussi,Marios Kountouris*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文从二阶散度展开出发，揭示散度正则化与SAM在参数扰动下局部一致，并扩展到输入扰动情形，用α-skew JSD验证曲率惩罚与泛化性能的关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 散度正则化与Sharpness-Aware Minimization都是基于扰动鲁棒性提升泛化的方法，但二者关系尚未被系统探讨。论文希望从局部几何视角统一理解这两种方法。

Method: 基于f-散度的经典二阶展开，分析参数空间扰动下散度正则化与SAM的局部一致性；对指数族输出分布的负对数似然目标，指出Fisher矩阵与Gauss-Newton矩阵重合。进一步将局部几何视角拓展至输入空间扰动，通过输入变换定义散度正则化并导出拉回二次型。以非对称α-skew Jensen-Shannon散度作为可控实验平台进行验证。

Result: 散度正则化产生Fisher加权二次型惩罚，SAM通过主Hessian特征值惩罚锐度，二者局部一致。α-skew JSD的局部曲率系数为α(1-α)，在对称点α=1/2处最大。损失景观可视化显示更强曲率惩罚对应更平坦的局部极小值；在四个基准数据集上，该最大曲率惩罚区域附近准确率与负对数似然均表现最佳。

Conclusion: 论文建立了散度正则化与SAM在参数和输入扰动下的统一局部几何解释，表明二者共享曲率敏感的扰动惩罚视角，并通过α-skew JSD实验验证了最大曲率惩罚区域与更好泛化性能的对应关系。

Abstract: Divergence-based regularization and Sharpness-Aware Minimization (SAM) are two prominent approaches for improving generalization in deep learning, both motivated by robustness to perturbations. However, their relationship has remained largely unexplored. Building on classical second-order expansions of $f$-divergences, we show that the two methods are locally consistent under parameter-space perturbations: both induce curvature-sensitive penalties, with divergence regularization yielding a Fisher-weighted quadratic form and SAM penalizing sharpness through the dominant Hessian eigenvalue. For negative log-likelihood objectives with exponential-family output distributions, this correspondence becomes especially transparent, since the Fisher and Gauss-Newton matrices coincide. We further show that the same local geometric perspective extends to input-space perturbations, where divergence-based regularization is defined through transformations of the input. In this setting, the regularizer induces a pullback quadratic form on the input space, providing a more general perturbation framework than standard SAM while preserving the same local sensitivity interpretation. To validate the analysis empirically, we use the asymmetric $α$-skew Jensen-Shannon divergence (JSD) family as a controlled testbed. Its local curvature coefficient scales as $α(1-α)$ and is maximized at the symmetric point $α=\tfrac12$, which recovers the standard JSD. Loss-landscape visualizations in the input-perturbation regime show that stronger induced curvature penalization is associated with flatter local minima. Experiments on four benchmark datasets further demonstrate that both accuracy and negative log-likelihood are consistently best near this regime of maximal curvature penalization.

</details>


### [208] [Constraint-Aware Discrete Black-Box Optimization Using Tensor Decomposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09370)
*Keisuke Onoue,Ryosuke Kojima*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于张量分解的代理模型，用于离散黑箱优化，通过将可行性逻辑约束直接整合进模型，以提升样本效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实中的离散黑箱优化问题，其可行输入常由事先已知的逻辑约束给出，但现有代理模型难以捕捉离散空间中支配可行性的符号规则，导致搜索效率低。

Method: 基于张量分解构建代理模型以捕捉离散搜索空间结构，并直接整合可行性信息；将代理模型训练表述为受约束的多项式优化问题，利用由T-范数导出的可微惩罚项求解其松弛形式。

Result: 在合成数据与真实基准（含压力容器设计任务）上的实验表明，该方法能有效引导搜索远离不可行区域，从而提升样本效率。

Conclusion: 将逻辑可行性约束与张量分解代理模型相结合，是提升离散黑箱优化样本效率的有效途径。

Abstract: Discrete black-box optimization is often addressed using approaches such as Sequential Model-Based Optimization (SMBO), which aims to improve sample efficiency by fitting surrogate models that approximate a costly objective function over a discrete search space. In many real-world problems, the set of feasible inputs is often given by logical constraints known in advance. However, existing surrogate modeling techniques generally fail to capture the symbolic rules governing feasibility in discrete input spaces. In this paper, we propose a surrogate modeling approach based on tensor decomposition that captures the structure of discrete search spaces while directly integrating feasibility information. To implement this approach, we formulate surrogate model training as a constrained polynomial optimization problem and solve a relaxed formulation using a differentiable penalty term derived from T-norms. Our experiments on both synthetic and real-world benchmarks, including a pressure vessel design task, demonstrate that the proposed method improves sample efficiency by effectively guiding the search away from infeasible regions.

</details>


### [209] [XAI-Refine: An Automated Explanation-Knowledge Loop for Brain-Age Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09388)
*Yang Qiao,Junjie Wu,Deqiang Qiu,James J. Lah,Liang Zhao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出XAI-Refine，一个自动化"解释-知识"闭环框架，用于基于静息态功能连接的脑龄预测：将可靠的事后解释转化为神经生物学问题，检索并核验文献，构建可采纳解释集，并以最小投影定义精修目标，在保持预测性能护栏下实现证据引导的模型修正。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 脑龄预测模型通常仅以预测精度评估，但高精度并不能保证模型依赖可复现或具神经生物学依据的机制。现有事后解释方法虽可揭示机制，但工作流往往止步于诊断，或需在分析前预先指定修正目标，缺乏自动化的证据引导修正路径。

Method: XAI-Refine为迭代式解释-知识闭环：每次迭代将多次重复训练中的互补事后分析整合为可靠、结构化的模型解释；将每条可靠解释转化为中立的神经生物学问题，检索并核验相关文献，并把已验证证据编译为同一类型化解释空间中的可采纳集；以当前模型解释在可采纳集上的最小投影作为精修目标，将修订后的解释转译为可微约束，同时保留原模型变量、测量算子与适用范围；仅当多种子验证确认解释朝目标移动、预测性能处于预设护栏内且非目标解释漂移有界时，才采纳候选更新。

Result: 在功能连接脑龄预测任务上开展了实验，评估了预测性能、解释可靠性、文献一致性以及目标特异性的模型修正效果，展示了从事后分析到证据引导模型精修的可行路径。

Conclusion: XAI-Refine为脑龄预测提供了从可靠事后解释到文献证据引导的自动模型精修的结构化流程，在维持预测性能的前提下提升了模型解释与神经生物学知识的一致性。

Abstract: Brain-age prediction models are commonly evaluated by predictive accuracy, yet accurate predictions alone do not establish that a model relies on reproducible or neurobiologically supported mechanisms. Post-hoc explanation methods can expose these mechanisms, but existing workflows typically stop at diagnosis or require correction targets to be specified before model analysis. We propose XAI-Refine, an automated explanation-knowledge loop for brain-age prediction from resting-state functional connectivity.
