At each iteration, XAI-Refine consolidates complementary post-hoc analyses across repeated training runs into reliable, structured model explanations. It converts each reliable explanation into a neutral neurobiological question, retrieves and verifies relevant literature, and compiles the verified evidence into an admissible set in the same typed explanation space. The target for refinement is defined as the minimal projection of the current model explanation onto the admissible set induced by applicable verified knowledge. This revised explanation is then translated into a differentiable constraint while preserving the originating model variable, measurement operator, and applicable scope. Candidate updates are promoted only when multi-seed validation confirms target-directed explanatory movement, predictive performance remains within a prespecified guardrail, and non-target explanatory drift remains bounded. Experiments on functional-connectivity-based brain-age prediction evaluate predictive performance, explanation reliability, literature alignment, and target-specific model revision, illustrating a structured route from post-hoc analysis to evidence-guided model refinement.

</details>


### [210] [Applying foundation model embeddings towards urban livability evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09429)
*Ayush Khot,Wen Zhou,Shaowen Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究地理空间基础模型（AlphaEarth、AnySat、TerraMind）嵌入中编码的物理特征，提出系统框架识别最具预测力的地理空间指标，用于数据稀缺地区的城市宜居性预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据稀缺地区的社会经济指标难以准确测量，限制了政策干预与资源分配；而高分辨率地理空间数据广泛可得且蕴含宜居性统计数据信息，因此有动机挖掘基础模型嵌入中的可用信息。

Method: 分析不同类型地理空间数据对城市宜居性预测的影响，评估基础模型（AlphaEarth、AnySat、TerraMind）嵌入所编码的物理特征，并构建系统框架识别最具预测力的地理空间指标，同时考虑复杂空间依赖性。

Result: 该框架使研究者能针对具体应用优先选择信息量最大的特征，并证明利用基础模型嵌入可提升宜居性相关结果的预测性能。

Conclusion: 本文提出了一套从卫星影像中提取可操作信息的原则性方法，能够应对复杂空间依赖，可应用于观测数据有限地区的城市宜居性预测。

Abstract: While accurate measurement of socioeconomic indicators remains challenging in data-scarce regions, which limits policy interventions and resource allocation, high-resolution geospatial data is widely available and can contain information on various livability statistics. We investigate which physical features are encoded within foundation model embeddings, such as AlphaEarth, AnySat, and TerraMind, and provide a systematic framework for identifying the most predictive geospatial indicators. By analyzing how different types of geospatial data influence urban livability predictions, our approach enables researchers to prioritize the most informative features for their specific applications. Additionally, we demonstrate how to leverage foundation model embeddings to enhance prediction performance for these outcomes. This work contributes a principled methodology for extracting actionable information from satellite imagery while accounting for complex spatial dependencies, with applications in predicting urban livability in regions with limited observation data.

</details>


### [211] [SCCM : Stream Cruise Control Method for Automated Drift Detection and Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09432)
*Mohammad Abu-Shaira,Weishi Shi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Stream Cruise Control Method (SCCM)框架，用于在线回归中的概念漂移检测与自适应，实现早期预警、漂移量化与动态超参数调节，并在多个数据集上取得更优预测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实数据分布常随时间演变（概念漂移），忽略漂移会降低预测性能，而依赖固定超参数进一步限制了模型在变化条件下的适应能力。现有纯被动方法通常只有在观测到性能下降后才启动适应，存在滞后问题。

Method: 提出SCCM框架，包含早期响应的预更新漂移检测、漂移幅度量化、基于KPI窗口阈值的局部误报缓解、动态超参数调优与模型重校准，并采用内存内设计支持实时适应；采用动态阈值且对数据分布保持不可知，可与多种在线回归模型集成。

Result: SCCM与四种在线回归模型集成，在覆盖突变、增量与交替渐变漂移的18个合成数据集及8个真实数据集上，以R2和MSE为指标，与八种检测器-适应基线比较，结果表明预测性能提升且漂移处理更有效。

Conclusion: SCCM为在线回归中的概念漂移检测与自适应提供了综合框架，能够在不同数据流（含高维与大规模场景）中实现实时、主动的适应，提升预测表现。

Abstract: Real-world datasets often exhibit evolving distributions, known as concept drift. Ignoring drift degrades predictive performance, while reliance on fixed hyperparameters further limits model adaptability under changing conditions. Adaptive learning addresses this challenge by continuously updating models online, allowing them to incrementally adjust and remain effective as data distributions evolve. This paper presents the Stream Cruise Control Method (SCCM), a comprehensive framework for drift detection and adaptation in online regression. SCCM enables automated adaptation through early-response, pre-update drift detection, drift magnitude quantification, KPI-window-based thresholding for local false-alarm mitigation, dynamic hyperparameter tuning, and model recalibration. SCCM also adopts an in-memory design for real-time adaptability, unlike purely reactive methods that typically activate adaptation only after performance degradation is observed. By using dynamic thresholding and remaining agnostic to data distributions, SCCM supports KPI-based monitoring across varying data streams, including high-dimensional and large-scale settings. SCCM is integrated with four online regression models and evaluated on 18 synthetic datasets covering abrupt, incremental, and alternating gradual drift, together with eight real-world datasets. The evaluation uses both R2 and MSE and compares against eight detector--adaptation baselines. Results show improved predictive performance and effective drift handling across the evaluated online regression settings.

</details>


### [212] [Efficient Leakage-Free Neural Architecture Search under Leave-One-Subject-Out Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09433)
*Heinke Hihn*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种无泄漏的块式NAS方法，通过跨受试者共享搜索过程，将LOSO评估下NAS的高计算代价大幅降低，在BioVid热痛数据集上精度略有提升且参数量减少最高99.2%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LOSO评估能估计基于受试者分类的泛化性能，但用于NAS时代价高昂：完全嵌套实现需要N次独立架构搜索，按近似线性训练成本计算复杂度达O(N^2)。

Method: 提出无泄漏、基于块的（block-based）方法，在受试者之间共享NAS搜索过程，避免独立重复搜索带来的二次复杂度。

Result: 在BioVid Heat Pain数据集上，平均准确率从82.79%提升至83.39%，同时参数量最多减少99.2%。

Conclusion: 该方法在保持无数据泄漏的前提下，以更低计算与参数成本实现了更优的LOSO泛化性能，为受试者级分类的NAS提供了高效方案。

Abstract: Leave-One-Subject-Out (LOSO) evaluation estimates generalisation performance for subject-based classification but makes Neural Architecture Search (NAS) computationally expensive because a fully nested implementation requires N independent architecture searches and, assuming approximately linear training cost, scales as O(N^2). We propose a leakage-free, block-based approach that shares NAS runs across subjects. On the BioVid Heat Pain dataset, our approach increased the mean accuracy from 82.79% to 83.39% while reducing the number of parameters by up to 99.2%.

</details>


### [213] [Tensor-Train Weak SINDy: Identifying High-Dimensional Nonlinear Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09434)
*Will Houser,Vanja Dukic,David M. Bortz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出TT-WSINDy方法，用张量列格式实现弱形式动力系统辨识，避免高维下的维数灾难。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近年来弱形式方法在数据驱动动力系统发现中取得显著进展，但在高维设置下，现有方法在计算和内存上都代价高昂，亟需高效的高维处理方案。

Method: 将多维非线性动力学近似（MANDy）与弱形式稀疏辨识（WSINDy）相结合，并以张量列（TT）格式实现弱形式变换、回归与稀疏化等所需计算。

Result: 该方法能够在指数级增长的候选函数空间中搜索，同时完成弱形式变换、回归与稀疏化，且不受维数灾难影响。

Conclusion: TT-WSINDy为高维数据驱动动力系统发现提供了一种高效可扩展的弱形式辨识方案。

Abstract: In recent years, weak-form methods have made significant advances in data-driven discovery of dynamical systems. However, in high-dimensional settings, current techniques can prove expensive in both computation and memory. In this work, we introduce TT-WSINDy, which combines techniques of the Multidimensional Approximation of Nonlinear Dynamics (MANDy) and Weak Sparse Identification of Nonlinear Dynamics (WSINDy) methods, implementing requisite computations in the tensor-train (TT) format. We demonstrate that this method is able to search an exponentially-growing space of candidate functions -- performing weak-form transformation, regression, and sparsification -- without suffering from the curse of dimensionality.

</details>


### [214] [Uncertainty-Aware Sea-Ice Type Mapping with Multiple Ice Charts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09451)
*Samira Alkaee Taleghan,Younghyun Koo,Andrew P. Barrett,Farnoush Banaei-Kashani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文研究海冰发展阶段（SoD）制图中的两类不确定性——多标注者标签不确定性与模型不确定性，并分析二者关系，发现软监督可提升二者对应性，尤其在冰边缘区域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SoD标签由人工分析卫星观测得到，不同冰情服务机构可能对相同冰况给出不同标签，这类“多标注者标签不确定性”常被合并为单一确定性目标而丢失。同时，学习模型本身也带来不确定性。论文动机是量化并关联这两类不确定性。

Method: 论文量化了来自独立冰情服务图表间分歧的标注不确定性，以及来自学习预测模型的模型不确定性，并检验模型不确定性是否在冰情服务分歧更大处更高；比较了不同监督方式，包括融合多标注者信息的软监督，以及Monte Carlo dropout等不确定性估计方法。

Result: 融合多标注者信息的监督能改善两类不确定性的对应关系，软监督取得最高总体相关性0.256；在冰边缘附近关系显著增强，0–10 km内相关性达0.704；Monte Carlo dropout给出最佳校准置信度，期望校准误差为0.050。

Conclusion: 多标注者标签不确定性与模型不确定性存在可量化的关联，且在冰边缘区域尤为紧密；采用软监督和合适的不确定性估计方法（如MC dropout）有助于更准确反映SoD制图中的不确定性。

Abstract: Sea-ice stage of development (SoD) describes the age and associated thickness of sea ice and provides important information for navigation, and operational ice monitoring. SoD labels are obtained from operational ice charts, where trained analysts interpret satellite observations and assign standardized stage codes to regions with similar ice conditions. These codes often represent ranges of compatible ice thicknesses rather than exact physical values. Deep-learning methods can automate SoD mapping and commonly adopt operational ice charts as reference labels for training. These annotations are not exact, however; this is because chart interpretation relies on analyst judgement and on the observations available at the time, so different ice services may assign different SoD labels to the same conditions. We term this variation across independently produced expert annotations multi-annotator label uncertainty; collapsing the annotations into a single deterministic target discards this variation. A second source of uncertainty originates in the learned model itself. In this paper, we quantify both sources: annotation uncertainty from disagreement among independent ice-service charts and model uncertainty from the learned predictive models. We then evaluate their relationship by testing whether model uncertainty is higher where ice services disagree. We observe that supervision incorporating information from multiple annotators can improve this correspondence, with soft supervision achieving the highest overall correlation of 0.256. The relationship becomes substantially stronger near the ice edge, where model predictive uncertainty closely tracks multi-annotator disagreement, reaching a correlation of 0.704 within 0--10 km. Among the uncertainty-estimation approaches, Monte Carlo dropout provides the best-calibrated confidence estimates, with an expected calibration error of 0.050.

</details>


### [215] [Exact-Form Regret for Gradient Descent, Mirror Descent and Follow-the-Regularized-Leader](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09466)
*Ashkan Soleymani,Gabriele Farina,Patrick Jaillet*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出"精确性"(exactness)作为统一几何原理，刻画在线梯度下降、镜像下降与FTRL相对于何种偏离类别可实现无遗憾，并在博弈中引入保守相关均衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统在线梯度下降研究多基于外部遗憾，即与固定基准比较。近期工作表明一阶方法能控制更丰富的、依赖动作的偏离，但缺乏对"这些算法究竟相对于哪些偏离类别无遗憾"的几何刻画。

Method: 论文引入"精确性"概念：相关位移场由标量势生成，或等价地所关联的一形式在算法所用几何中为精确形式。该几何随算法而变——梯度下降为欧氏几何，镜像下降为由正则项诱导的几何，FTRL为累积对偶状态。作者在温和正则条件下分析精确性与遗憾的关系，并研究精确形式偏离诱导的均衡概念，提出"保守相关均衡"。

Result: 在温和正则条件下，精确性可导出次线性遗憾；而非零环量(非精确)构成互补的障碍并导致线性遗憾。由此为不同一阶方法所控制的偏离类别给出统一几何框架，并揭示不同方法可控制本质不同的偏离类别。在博弈中刻画了保守相关均衡与相关均衡的关系，确定了二者何时重合、何时分离，以及这些关系如何依赖几何与学习算法。

Conclusion: 本文为理解一阶在线学习算法"相对于什么无遗憾"(超越固定比较基准)提供了统一的几何解释，并说明偏离类别对学习特别是博弈具有直接影响。

Abstract: Online gradient descent is usually studied through external regret, where the learner competes with fixed alternatives. Recent work shows that first-order methods control richer action-dependent deviations. We ask for a geometric characterization of the deviations with respect to which online gradient descent, mirror descent, and follow-the-regularized-leader (FTRL) achieve no regret. We identify exactness as the common principle. Exactness means that the relevant displacement field is generated by a scalar potential, or equivalently that the associated one-form is exact in the geometry used by the algorithm. This geometry depends on the algorithm. For gradient descent it is Euclidean geometry, for mirror descent it is the geometry induced by the regularizer, and for FTRL it is the cumulative dual state. Under mild regularity conditions, exactness yields sublinear regret, while nonzero circulation provides the complementary obstruction and leads to linear regret. This gives a unified geometric framework for understanding the deviation classes controlled by these algorithms and reveals that different first-order methods can control genuinely different classes of deviations. These deviation classes have direct consequences for learning, particularly in games. We study the equilibrium notions induced by exact-form deviations and introduce conservative correlated equilibrium, reflecting both the conservative geometry of the underlying displacement fields and the restricted family of deviations available to the players. We characterize its relation to correlated equilibrium, determine when the resulting equilibrium notions coincide and when they separate, and show how these relationships depend on the geometry and the learning algorithm. Overall, this work gives a unified geometric account of what first-order online learning algorithms are no-regret with respect to, beyond fixed comparators.

</details>


### [216] [Building the Harness Automatically: Self-Play in Code Distills a Text Harness for Black-Box Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09468)
*Yi Wu,Zheng Ren,Zhiyu Hu,Haochen Wang,Daryl Chang,Li Wei,Ting Wang,Zhen Li,Pooja Gupta,Nitin Jindal,Lukasz Heldt*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出一种框架：智能体通过反复编写和评估优化器程序进行"可执行练习"，并将学到的搜索策略蒸馏为一段197词的文本（Harness A），冻结后在黑箱优化任务中显著提升多个LLM的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低预算黑箱优化中，未经辅助的语言模型表现远低于强经典优化器，亟需一种方法让模型习得有效的数值搜索策略。

Method: 智能体在开发阶段反复编写并评估优化器程序，随后将所得程序与练习记录一次性蒸馏成197词的主文本Harness A并在评估前冻结；通过独立复现生成Harness B，并在YouTube奖励调优生产基准上验证。

Result: Harness A使Gemini Flash的regret降低48%（N=30，p<.001），进入GP-BO性能区间，并在三个留出的BBOB景观上降低平均regret；该文本改善所有测试的Gemini执行器并迁移至Claude Sonnet（regret降低43%和49%，p≤.005）；Harness B达到同等级性能；在封存的YouTube奖励调优生产基准上取得最低regret。

Conclusion: 可执行练习是发现搜索策略的可行途径，而语言是可移植的策略部署媒介。

Abstract: Can an agent learn a numerical search strategy through executable practice and then transfer that strategy as text? We study low-budget black-box optimization, where unaided language models remain well below strong classical optimizers. During development, an agent repeatedly writes and evaluates optimizer programs. It then distills the resulting program and practice record once into a 197-word primary Harness A, which is frozen before evaluation. Harness A reduces Gemini Flash regret by 48\% in an independent $N=30$ study ($p<.001$), enters the GP-BO performance range on the practice family, and lowers mean regret on all three held-out BBOB landscapes. The same text improves every tested Gemini executor and transfers to Claude Sonnet, reducing regret by 43\% and 49\% ($p\leq.005$). An independent end-to-end replication produces Harness B, a different program and text at the same performance tier. The same framework also attains the lowest regret on a sealed YouTube reward-tuning production benchmark. Executable practice is thus a viable way to discover a search policy, and language a portable medium for deploying it.

</details>


### [217] [From Fixed Keys to Readable Schemas: Small Language Models for Vehicle Agent Function Calls](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09476)
*Hamed Jafarzadeh Asl,Yuanhao Yu,Vahid Partovi Nia*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文研究车载智能助手中的小语言模型函数调用，比较功能令牌（FT）与提示内模式（SIP）两种函数呈现方式，发现函数呈现方式而非模型规模决定SLM的能力与失败模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 车载助手需在严格内存和延迟约束下将自然语言请求转为准确的车载功能调用，因此小语言模型（SLM）适合端侧部署。关键设计选择是如何呈现可用函数接口，即用专门的功能令牌（FT）还是直接在提示中给出函数模式（SIP）。

Method: 作者构建了一个来自 Android Automotive 的基准，包含 9,822 个单轮样例、覆盖 79 个车载功能，并包含留出函数和需拒绝的请求。在四个参数规模从 270M 到 1.7B 的 SLM 上，以匹配的微调设置对比 FT 和 SIP 两种方法，并进行理论分析。

Result: 在训练中见过的函数上，规模收益有限：270M 模型可匹配 1.7B 模型，整体最强性能出现在 0.6B。在留出函数上，FT 按构造为零准确率，SIP 能泛化且随规模显著提升。在范围外请求上，FT 可能调用其被训练输出的不可用函数，SIP 则更能基于所提供函数可靠拒绝。SIP 的灵活性伴随更高内存占用和延迟。

Conclusion: 理论分析解释了 SIP 为何能泛化以及更长的模式上下文为何增加推理成本。总体而言，函数接口的表示方式而非单纯模型规模，决定了基于 SLM 的车载函数调用的能力和失败模式。该论文属于 LLM（大语言模型）方向。

Abstract: In-vehicle assistants must translate natural-language requests into accurate vehicle function calls under strict memory and latency constraints, making small language models (SLMs) attractive for on-device deployment. For such models, a key design choice is how the available function surface is presented. Two approaches are to represent each function with a dedicated Functional Token (FT) or provide function schemas directly in the prompt. FTs enable compact inference but are restricted to functions learned during training, whereas Schema-in-Prompt (SIP) can generalize to unseen functions at the cost of longer prompts and higher inference overhead. We introduce a benchmark of 9,822 single-turn examples spanning 79 vehicle functions derived from Android Automotive, including held-out functions and requests requiring refusal. We compare both approaches under matched fine-tuning across four SLMs from 270M to 1.7B parameters. On functions seen during training, scaling provides limited benefit: the 270M model can match the 1.7B model, while the strongest overall performance occurs at 0.6B. On held-out functions, FT achieves zero accuracy by construction, whereas SIP generalizes and improves substantially with scale. On out-of-scope requests, FT can invoke an unavailable function it was trained to emit, while SIP more reliably refuses based on the functions offered. This flexibility comes with higher memory use and latency. Our theoretical analysis explains how SIP enables generalization and why longer schema contexts increase inference cost. Overall, function-surface representation, rather than model scale alone, determines the capabilities and failure modes of SLM-based vehicle function calling.

</details>


### [218] [Unthrottling the Tanh Jacobian in SAC: A Negative Result on Bang-Bang Control and MetaDrive](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09478)
*Faiq Shamass*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文检验了SAC中tanh压缩导致动作边界处雅可比消失（梯度"节流"）是否真的损害学习：结果显示该效应确实存在，但用额外旁路梯度去"修复"它非但无益，反而有害。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SAC将无界高斯策略经tanh压缩到有界动作区间，其雅可比∂a/∂u=1−a²在|a|→1时趋于零，因此有人担心在最优动作位于边界（全刹车、全油门等bang-bang最优）时，actor无法从critic获得有效信号。本文旨在验证这一担忧是否成立，并检验补偿该信号是否能提升性能。

Method: 提出一种最小干预：在actor损失中加入一项额外项，使得在pre-tanh均值上的梯度等于Q的detached动作梯度，且不引入增益参数。设计两种变体进行对比：(1)无门控旁路（ungated bypass）；(2)仅在平坦肩部|a|∈[0.9,0.999]触发的门控旁路（gated bypass）。实验一：最小时间双重积分器，最优解为边界bang-bang控制，使用十个配对随机种子与标定最优值−30.3对比。实验二：热启动的MetaDrive微调任务。

Result: 在双重积分器上，原始SAC已达近最优回报−31.6（最优−30.3）；无门控旁路使99%评估步的动作|a|≥0.9、策略饱和，回报崩溃至−195.5；门控旁路同样失败，但并未产生饱和策略。MetaDrive微调呈相同模式：旁路未提升回报，碰撞率下降往往以偏离道路为代价；自动调节的熵系数也随之上升，将策略推向尾部。

Conclusion: 雅可比节流效应确实存在，但将其视为需要修复的bug并不划算，在所研究任务上也无帮助。触碰边界并饱和与真正求解一个最优解位于边界的问题，是两件不同的事。

Abstract: Soft Actor-Critic (SAC) represents a continuous policy as an unbounded Gaussian that is squashed by tanh. The Jacobian of that map is $\partial a/\partial u = 1-a^2$, which vanishes as $|a|\to 1$. A natural concern is that this throttle starves the actor of critic signal exactly where extreme actions (full brake, full throttle) are optimal. We test a minimal intervention that restores the missing signal: one extra term in the actor loss whose gradient on the pre-tanh mean is the detached action-gradient of $Q$, with no gain parameter.
  On a minimum-time double integrator whose optimum is bang-bang at the action bounds, vanilla SAC already reaches near-optimal return ($-31.6$ vs. a calibrated optimum of $-30.3$) across ten paired seeds. An ungated bypass does saturate the policy (99% of eval steps with $|a|\ge 0.9$) and collapses return to $-195.5$. A gated bypass that fires only on the flat shoulder $|a|\in[0.9,0.999]$ also fails, and does so without leaving a saturated policy. Warm-started MetaDrive fine-tuning shows the same pattern: the bypass does not improve return, and where collision rate falls it is typically traded for out-of-road departures. Auto-tuned entropy coefficient rises against the bypass, which is a push toward the tails.
  The Jacobian effect is real. Treating it as a bug to be undone is not free, and on the tasks studied here it is not helpful. Saturating a bound is not the same as solving a problem whose optimum lives on that bound.

</details>


### [219] [Efficient Fairness Auditing Across Guidance Scales in Text-to-Image Diffusion Models via Causal Abstraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09486)
*Nabila Tasfiha Rahman,Rajatsubhra Chakraborty,Depeng Xu,Lu Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出基于因果抽象的审计工具，用概率transformer以摊销方式预测不同引导尺度下的目标特征分布，从而高效评估文本到图像扩散模型的公平性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文本到图像扩散模型的公平性审计通常需要在多种采样配置下生成大量图像，计算成本高昂。作者希望降低这一成本，实现对引导尺度（classifier-free guidance scale）干预下公平性的高效评估。

Method: 将扩散过程建模为低层结构因果模型，并在抽象的去噪状态上构建对应的高层模型；刻画投影因果结构，证明公平相关干预查询的可识别性，并给出高层模型保持该查询的充分条件；用一个概率transformer实现高层模型，作为跨引导尺度的目标特征分布摊销预测器。

Result: 实验评估了分布保真度、公平性查询准确度与计算效率；给出两个审计演示，分别使用标准 Stable Diffusion 1.5 和公平性增强模型 StayFair，考察它们在多引导尺度下的行为。

Conclusion: 该因果抽象审计框架可在显著降低计算开销的同时，较准确地评估扩散模型在不同引导尺度下的公平性，为扩散模型公平性审计提供了高效可行的方法。

Abstract: Fairness auditing of text-to-image diffusion models often requires generating large numbers of images across sampling configurations, making comprehensive evaluation computationally expensive. We propose a causal-abstraction-based audit instrument for efficiently evaluating fairness under interventions on the classifier-free guidance scale. Given a fixed prompt and a target feature function, we represent the diffusion process as a low-level structural causal model and construct a corresponding high-level model over abstract denoising states. We characterize the projected causal structure, establish identifiability of the fairness-relevant interventional query, and provide sufficient conditions under which the high-level model preserves this query. A probabilistic transformer implements the high-level model as an amortized predictor of target-feature distributions across guidance scales. Experiments evaluate distributional fidelity, fairness-query accuracy, and computational efficiency. We present two auditing demonstrations: one using standard Stable Diffusion 1.5 and another using StayFair, a fairness-enhanced Stable Diffusion model, to examine their behavior across guidance scales.

</details>


### [220] [A Statistical Approach to Estimating Sample Size of Machine Learning Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09547)
*Dat Phan-Trong,Sunil Gupta,Svetha Venkatesh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一个通过局部线性近似估计非线性机器学习预测模型样本量需求的框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非线性ML模型学习复杂预测曲面，传统功效分析难以直接进行解析计算，而样本量确定又需要预先指定预测变量与结局的关系，因此缺乏可行方法。

Method: 用局部线性表示近似非线性ML模型，并在各局部区域上评估统计功效，从而估计样本量需求。

Result: 摘要未提供具体实验结果或数值。

Conclusion: 该框架为非线性ML预测模型的样本量确定提供了可行途径。

Abstract: Sample size determination for machine learning (ML) prediction models is challenging because conventional power analysis typically requires the predictor-outcome relationship and effect structure to be specified a priori. Nonlinear ML models learn complex prediction surfaces that do not admit straightforward analytical power calculations. We propose a framework that approximates nonlinear ML models with localized linear representations and estimates sample size requirements by evaluating statistical power across these local regions.

</details>


### [221] [Robust Industrial Cyber Physical Classification Using Neuromorphic Temporal Embeddings and Hybrid SNN XGBoost Under Machine Unlearning Attacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09564)
*Ammar Kamoona,Sajad Koushkbaghi,Mahdi Jalili,Peter McTaggart,Xinghuo Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种SNN+XGBoost混合架构用于电力配电网入侵检测，兼顾边缘部署效率与抗数据投毒/机器遗忘攻击的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电力配电网数字化使其基础设施更易遭受网络攻击，但现有入侵检测系统依赖计算昂贵的深度学习模型，难以在边缘部署；同时周期性重训练会使其面临机器遗忘攻击，选择性删除数据即可降低检测性能。因此需要一种既轻量又具备结构鲁棒性的检测方案。

Method: 采用脉冲神经网络（SNN）与XGBoost的混合架构：SNN在干净数据上一次性训练后作为固定特征提取器，提供高效的时间编码；更新时仅重训练XGBoost这一轻量级分类器，从而避免全模型重训练带来的攻击面。

Result: 在两个真实公开电力系统数据集上评估：Synchrophasor数据集达99.9%准确率（F1-macro 0.999），MSU/ORNL数据集达95.0%准确率（F1-macro 0.943），均优于单模型基线。在选择性标签翻转攻击下，10%投毒时混合模型F1-macro仅下降0.9%，并将目标类崩溃阈值从60%投毒延迟至70%。

Conclusion: 神经形态时间编码可为信息物理系统同时提供高精度网络攻击检测和更强的抗数据投毒能力，是一种适用于边缘部署的鲁棒IDS方案。

Abstract: The digitalisation of electrical distribution networks has increased the exposure of power-grid infrastructure to cyber attacks. Existing intrusion detection systems (IDSs), however, often rely on computationally expensive deep learning models that are difficult to deploy at the edge. Periodic retraining also exposes these systems to machine unlearning attacks, where selective data removal can degrade detection performance. We propose a hybrid Spiking Neural Network (SNN) and XGBoost architecture that combines efficient temporal encoding with a lightweight classifier and provides structural resilience to such attacks. The SNN is trained once on clean data and used as a fixed feature extractor, while only the XGBoost classifier is retrained during model updates. Evaluated on two real-world public power-system datasets, the proposed method achieves 99.9\% accuracy (F1-macro 0.999) on the Synchrophasor dataset and 95.0\% accuracy (F1-macro 0.943) on the MSU/ORNL dataset, outperforming standalone baselines. Under selective label-flipping attacks, the hybrid model loses only 0.9\% F1-macro at 10\% poisoning and delays target-class collapse from 60\% to 70\% poisoning compared with raw models. These results demonstrate that neuromorphic temporal encoding can provide both accurate cyber-attack detection and improved resilience to data poisoning in cyber-physical systems.

</details>


### [222] [Positional task conditioning for scalable defect detection across product families in large product catalogs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09567)
*Soham Satyadharma,Gabriel Roccabruna,Suleiman A. Khan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文针对大型产品目录中产品家族存在的重复项与单位不匹配等不一致问题，提出将检测任务分解为聚焦子任务以缓解长上下文限制，并引入位置任务条件化（PTC）将能力蒸馏到单个小模型，实现低成本可扩展部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型产品目录中的产品家族常出现重复、单位不匹配等不一致，损害客户体验。检测需在冗长产品列表中跨多种错误类型进行推理，而LLM因长上下文限制导致分类质量下降，同时规模化部署对成本有较高要求。

Method: 将检测任务分解为聚焦的子任务，以减少上下文长度并隔离不同错误类型；进一步提出位置任务条件化（PTC），通过在结构化提示边界强化任务身份，将该能力蒸馏到单个更小的模型中。

Result: 任务分解使F1从52%提升至87%；PTC在五种模型、两个架构家族上均优于基于理由的蒸馏方法，与前沿模型F1差距在1.79%以内，成本降低最高达98%。

Conclusion: 该方法已在多个国家部署，处理超过1000万个产品家族，验证了其在真实大规模工业场景中的有效性与可扩展性。

Abstract: Product families in large product catalogs suffer from inconsistencies such as duplicates and unit mismatches that degrade customer experience. Detecting these requires reasoning over multiple error types across lengthy product listings, where LLM classification quality degrades due to long-context limitations. We address this by decomposing detection into focused sub-tasks that reduce context and isolate error types, improving F1 from 52\% to 87\%. For scalable deployment, we introduce Positional Task Conditioning (PTC), which distills this capability into a single smaller model by reinforcing task identity at structural prompt boundaries. PTC outperforms rationale-based distillation across five models and two architecture families, achieving within 1.79\% F1 of the frontier at upto 98\% lower cost. Our system is deployed across multiple countries processing 10+ million product families.

</details>


### [223] [Teacher Geometry Shapes Learnability in Teacher-Student Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09595)
*Kai J. Sandbrink,Flavio Martinelli,Alexander van Meegen,Wulfram Gerstner,Johanni Brea*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文研究教师-学生神经网络中教师结构对可学习性的影响，发现教师几何分布（节点相似度）显著改变收敛成功率，并通过损失景观分析解释其机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统教师-学生系统研究常假设教师参数为随机正态分布，忽略了教师结构差异，掩盖了不同教师可学习性的巨大变化。论文希望将可学习性形式化，并揭示教师几何对学习难度的影响。

Method: 将可学习性定义为收敛到全局最小值的成功率，作为过参数化、学习算法、学生初始化分布和教师几何的函数；识别出最大化节点不相似度的“易”分布和最小化节点不相似度的“难”分布；在无限数据和快速读出层假设下，将小型网络损失景观解析降到二维，分析两类次优局部极小值——数据分布边缘的越界（OOB）极小值和内部极小值。

Result: 最大不相似度与最小不相似度的教师分布在各种设置和激活函数下产生显著不同的成功率；较大网络中，最大不相似度教师诱导更多内部极小值，最小不相似度教师诱导更多OOB极小值；基于此，差分地提高读出层学习率并降低内部偏置学习率可提升成功率。

Conclusion: 教师几何结构是决定可学习性的关键因素，损失景观中内部与OOB极小值的分布随教师结构变化；该研究缩小了教师-学生网络研究与实际中更结构化函数之间的差距。

Abstract: Teacher-student systems, in which a teacher neural network generates training labels so that a student neural network can learn to implement the same function, are widely used as an abstract setting to study learning. However, the structure of the teachers is often overlooked by assuming randomly-generated, normally-distributed parameters. This hides substantial variation in how learnable different teachers are. We formalize learnability as the success rate of converging to the global minimum, as a function of overparameterization, learning algorithm, student initialization distribution, and teacher geometry. We both identify an easy distribution that maximizes node dissimilarity and a hard distribution that minimizes it, and show that these two distributions induce markedly different success rates across a large range of settings and for different activation functions. To explain the gap, we study the loss landscape of small neural networks that contain two distinct kinds of suboptimal local minima, out-of-bounds (OOB) minima at the edge of the data distribution and interior minima within. Assuming infinite data and a fast readout layer, we analytically reduce the loss landscape of small networks to two dimensions, showing that the region of attraction of interior minima changes as a function of teacher structure. In larger networks, maximally dissimilar teachers induce more interior minima, while minimally dissimilar teachers induce more OOB minima. Motivated by these analyses, we show that differentially increasing the learning rate of the readout layer and decreasing the learning rate of the inner biases increases success rates. These findings provide an important step in narrowing the gap between the study of teacher-student networks and more structured functions that arise in practice.

</details>


### [224] [Cascading Gradient Inversion via LT-Code Inspired Peeling in Federated Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09659)
*Saeed Shariati,Mohsen Alambardar Meybodi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文将梯度反演与纠删码理论建立联系，构造出可从单轮FedSGD精确恢复整批训练样本及其标签的重建攻击，突破了已知上界，表明联邦学习的隐私泄露被严重低估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习共享模型更新而非原始数据，但更新可被反演以重建客户端训练数据。现有解析式重建攻击在批次增大时性能急剧下降：即便攻击者完全控制网络参数，单轮攻击对批次大小100也仅能恢复约一半样本，且已有上界限制了这类方法的能力。

Method: 作者建立了梯度反演与纠删码理论的联系，并据此构造新的攻击方法：从单轮FedSGD中精确恢复整批样本及每个样本的标签，且无需真实数据即可对每次恢复进行认证。方法同时适用于被动攻击者（仅观察正常训练的网络）和主动攻击者（可操纵模型参数）。

Result: 在八个图像与表格基准上，该方法大幅超越先前单轮攻击。被动攻击者可在批次大小达128时恢复94%–100%的ImageNet批次，超过先前攻击在主动操纵模型下的表现；主动设置下批次大小达数百时仍恢复超过90%。

Conclusion: 研究表明联邦学习的隐私泄露程度被低估，现有关于梯度反演可恢复样本数量的上界需要重新审视，联邦学习部署中的隐私保护需进一步加强。

Abstract: Federated learning shares model updates rather than raw data, yet these updates can be inverted to reconstruct the clients' training data. Analytic reconstruction attacks, which invert a gradient in closed form, degrade as the batch grows: prior single-round attacks recover only about half of a batch of size $100$ even when the attacker fully controls the network parameters, and known upper bounds limit what any such method can recover. We establish a connection between gradient inversion and the theory of erasure-correcting codes, and use it to construct attacks that exceed these bounds. Our attacks recover batches exactly, together with every sample's label, from a single FedSGD round, and certify each recovery without ground-truth data. On eight image and tabular benchmarks they outperform prior single-round attacks by a wide margin. Even a passive attacker who only observes an honestly trained network recovers $94$--$100\%$ of ImageNet batches at sizes up to $128$, more than prior single-round attacks achieve even with active manipulation of the model, and in the active setting more than $90\%$ is recovered at batch sizes of several hundred. These results show that the privacy leakage of federated learning has been underestimated.

</details>


### [225] [PELM: Power Efficient On-Device LLM Inference with Speculative Decoding and Dynamic Voltage Frequency Scaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09662)
*Weisi Yang,Stephen Xia*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出PELM方法，通过在传统DVFS频率调节基础上增加推测解码与可变验证深度两个工作负载调节维度，扩展优化空间，实现更节能的端侧LLM推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将LLM直接部署在移动与边缘平台可带来隐私增强、个性化与低延迟等好处，但LLM计算需求巨大，资源受限的移动平台难以满足。此外移动设备形态紧凑，缺乏散热手段（如风扇），高负载易导致降频与性能下降。已有DVFS方法主要优化硬件参数与处理器频率，在受热约束场景下效果不足。

Method: 基于"并非所有token都需要全深度推理即可维持高质量生成"这一洞察，PELM在传统DVFS处理器频率调节之外，引入两个工作负载相关调节旋钮：1）推测解码（speculative decoding）；2）可变验证深度（variable verification depth），从而将优化空间扩展到多个维度，实现更节能的端侧LLM推理。

Result: 在多种硬件平台与数据集上的广泛评估中，PELM优于当前最先进的功耗管理方法，最高实现23.1%的加速和52.4%的能耗降低，同时保持相当的任务性能。

Conclusion: PELM通过将DVFS与工作负载层面的推测解码及可变验证深度相结合，有效缓解移动端LLM推理的功耗与散热瓶颈，在保证生成质量的前提下显著提升能效。

Abstract: Deploying Large Language Models (LLMs) directly on mobile platforms at the edge is gaining traction due to a myriad of benefits, such as increased privacy, personalization, and reduced latency. However, LLMs have heavy computational requirements, which are difficult for resource-constrained mobile and edge platforms to fulfill. In addition to limited compute resources, mobile and edge systems often have a compact form factor and lack physical mechanisms to dissipate heat generated from high processor usage rates (e.g., fans) to prevent throttling and reduced processing power, which LLMs can easily cause. To mitigate these effects, prior works have proposed various power governing strategies, such as dynamic voltage and frequency scaling (DVFS), for reducing power and heat generation for heavy computational tasks on mobile platforms. Recently, DVFS methods tailored for mobile LLMs have also been proposed. However, these methods mostly focus on optimizing hardware parameters and processor frequencies, and they fall short under some thermally constrained scenarios. Drawing from recent advances in machine learning, we identify and take advantage of the key insight that not all tokens require full-depth inference to maintain high-quality generation. Motivated by this, we present PELM, a solution that augments traditional DVFS processor frequency tuning with two additional workload-specific knobs: 1) speculative decoding and 2) variable verification depth to expand the optimization space to multiple dimensions for more power efficient on-device LLM inference. In extensive evaluations across hardware platforms and datasets, PELM demonstrates superior performance compared to state-of-the-art power governing methods, with up to 23.1% speedup and 52.4% reduction in energy consumption, while maintaining comparable task performance. The source code is available at https://github.com/imec-nu/PELM.

</details>


### [226] [Muon-C: Operator-Aligned Muon for Convolutional Kernels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09676)
*Jiaxin Qing,Lexin Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Muon-C将卷积核动量表示为频域通道传递矩阵并分块正交化，构造与卷积算子对齐的优化几何，在CIFAR-10流匹配上以更少FLOPs显著优于Muon与Adam。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Muon用近似正交的极向方向替代矩阵动量，但其几何依赖矩阵表示形式；对卷积而言，标准展开（unfolding）描述的是局部patch映射而非卷积算子本身，因此几何与真实算子不匹配。

Method: 提出Muon-C：将卷积核动量表示为按频率划分的通道传递矩阵块，对各块独立做极分解（正交化），并借助临界Fourier网格把更新精确映射回原有限核支撑。理论上说明新几何来自块划分与Fourier坐标的结合，其精确极向方向在临界采样卷积范数下是线性最小化预言机。

Result: 最坏情况下相对连续卷积算子范数的保证不弱于展开方法，且对3×3核严格更强。CIFAR-10流匹配上，匹配更新RMS时40k迭代达9.87 FID（unfolded Muon为22.26，Adam为51.31）；达到其最终质量仅需0.62×和0.64×的模型FLOPs。同等调参预算下Muon-C达3.42 FID，收益在多种数据规模下保持并迁移到卷积架构的分类任务。

Conclusion: 按卷积算子几何对齐的动量正交化（Muon-C）比矩阵展开式Muon更符合卷积结构，理论上保证更强、实践中收敛更快且质量更高，并可推广至不同数据规模与卷积分类任务。

Abstract: Muon replaces matrix momentum with an approximately orthogonal polar direction, but its geometry depends on the matrix representation. For convolution, standard unfolding describes a local patch map rather than the convolution operator. We introduce Muon-C, an operator-aligned optimizer that represents kernel momentum as frequency-wise channel-transfer matrices, polarizes these blocks independently, and uses a critical Fourier grid to return updates exactly to the original finite kernel support. We show that the new geometry arises from combining the block partition and Fourier coordinates. The exact-polar direction is a linear minimization oracle under the critically sampled convolution norm. Its worst-case guarantee relative to the continuous convolution-operator norm is never weaker than unfolding and is strictly stronger for $3\times3$ kernels. On CIFAR-10 flow matching with matched applied-update RMS, Muon-C reaches 9.87 FID at 40k iterations, compared with 22.26 for unfolded Muon and 51.31 for Adam. It reaches their final quality using $0.62\times$ and $0.64\times$ their model FLOPs, respectively. Under equal tuning budgets, Muon-C achieves 3.42 FID. Gains persist across data scales and transfer to classification across convolutional architectures.

</details>


### [227] [Settling: Equilibrium Inference for Non-Convex Validity Sets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09682)
*Lyes Saad Saoud*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文指出回归系统输出单点估计时，在非凸/不连通可行集上会出现"条件均值崩溃"，并提出基于平衡的推理算子 Settling 加以解决。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多学习系统即便在可行输出为非凸或不连通集合时，仍只返回单点估计。在平方损失下，模糊的条件分布其贝叶斯最优条件均值可能是无效解，这一失效被作者形式化为"条件均值崩溃"。

Method: 提出 Settling，一种基于平衡的推理算子，将候选生成、一致性评估与测试时平衡选择分离：以均值寻求的候选作为初始化，逐步精炼至局部稳定构型；给定初始化后精炼过程是确定性的。理论上给出精确梯度下降、局部收敛性，以及适用于学习一致性评价器的非精确梯度鲁棒性条件。

Result: 在可复现的 100 上下文几何诊断中，均值寻求基线成功率 0/100，随机去噪 100/100，Settling 为 99/100 且轨迹粗糙度显著更低。1200 次敏感性研究中，障碍抖动至 0.20 时成功率 97–100%，一次性初始化扰动 0.05–0.50 时成功率 94–100%。

Conclusion: Settling 能有效避免条件均值崩溃并保持高成功率与稳定轨迹；但跨域对比仅为机制示例，学习型高维验证仍是未决的实证问题。

Abstract: Many learning systems return a single point estimate even when admissible outputs form disconnected or non-convex sets. Under squared loss, an ambiguous conditional distribution can therefore have a Bayes-optimal conditional mean that is invalid. We formalize this failure as conditional mean collapse and introduce Settling, an equilibrium-based inference operator that separates proposal generation, consistency evaluation, and test-time equilibrium selection. The operator treats a mean-seeking proposal as an initialization and refines it toward a locally stable configuration; conditional on initialization, refinement is deterministic. We establish exact-gradient descent, local convergence, and an inexact-gradient robustness condition relevant to learned consistency critics. In a reproducible 100-context geometric diagnostic, the mean-seeking baseline succeeds in 0/100 contexts, stochastic denoising in 100/100, and Settling in 99/100 while producing substantially lower trajectory roughness. A 1,200-run sensitivity study yields 97-100% success across obstacle-jitter ranges up to 0.20 and 94-100% across one-time initialization perturbations from 0.05 to 0.50. Cross-domain panels remain mechanism illustrations; learned high-dimensional validation remains an open empirical test.

</details>


### [228] [ALIGN-HOLD: Experience Alignment for Real-Time Hold Control in Large-Scale Ride-Hailing Matching at DiDi](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09685)
*Zuhao Zhang,Xu Liu,Kai Wan,Zihao Lu,Li Ma,Shuai Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 ALIGN-HOLD，一个从隐式市场偏好学习 hold 策略的生产级经验对齐框架，并在 DiDi 巴西平台完成大规模在线部署验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实时 hold 控制对网约车平台的匹配效率至关重要，但现有生产系统依赖人工设计的奖励组合，面对异构偏好、稀疏噪声反馈和动态供需条件时难以扩展。

Method: 构建订单轨迹、司机轨迹与同期局部匹配图生成的互补偏好对，训练经验奖励模型（RM），采用平衡多视角采样和模型自适应困难偏好采样；在仿真策略学习中用冻结 RM 提供密集的场景相关奖励，并支持对低可辨识交互的无标签过滤。

Result: 在 DiDi 平台进行 28 天随机 A/B 实验，每日约 10 万次乘客请求；相比生产策略，行程完成率和司机收入有显著提升，接单前后乘客取消显著下降；消融、RM 诊断与行为分析验证各组件贡献。

Conclusion: ALIGN-HOLD 已全量上线，当前服务 DiDi 巴西市场，验证了经验对齐方法在真实大规模出行场景中的有效性。

Abstract: Real-time hold control is a high-leverage mechanism in large-scale ride-hailing systems: by selectively deferring driver-order pairs, the platform can wait for better matching opportunities and improve end-to-end passenger-driver experience. Existing production systems such as EXHOLD learn bandit-based hold policies from handcrafted combinations of trip completion, cancellations, waiting time, and driver effort. However, designing such rewards becomes increasingly difficult as marketplace preferences are heterogeneous and observed passenger-driver behavior can be sparse, noisy, and affected by dynamic supply-demand conditions.
  We present ALIGN-HOLD, a production-scale experience alignment framework that learns hold policy from implicit marketplace preferences. ALIGN-HOLD constructs complementary preference pairs from order trajectories, driver trajectories, and contemporaneous local matching graphs, and trains an experience Reward Model (RM) using balanced multi-view sampling and model-adaptive hard preference sampling. During simulator-based policy learning, the frozen RM provides a dense, context-dependent reward and supports label-free filtering of low-identifiability interactions whose behavioral feedback is difficult to attribute to matching quality.
  We deploy ALIGN-HOLD on DiDi's ride-hailing platform and evaluate it in a 28-day randomized A/B experiment, covering approximately 100,000 passenger requests per day. Compared with the deployed production policy, ALIGN-HOLD achieves statistically significant improvements in trip completion rate and driver income, while significantly reducing passenger cancellations before and after driver acceptance. Complementary ablations, RM diagnostics, and behavioral analyses validate the contributions of the proposed components. ALIGN-HOLD has been fully ramped up and is currently serving DiDi's Brazil marketplace.

</details>


### [229] [Kernel-Complexity Edge Sanitization for Training-Free Defense against Structural Graph Attacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09698)
*Yaning Jia,Shenyang Deng,Yaoqing Yang,Chiyu Ma,Wenxuan Xu,Soroush Vosoughi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 KCES，一种免训练、模型无关的图神经网络结构攻击防御框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 GNN 对抗防御缺乏理论支撑、依赖特定攻击启发式或需昂贵重训练。

Method: 基于图 Gram 矩阵导出图核复杂度 GKC 与泛化上界，定义边级 KC 分数并剪除高 KC 边。

Result: 在多种攻击设置下持续优于代表性鲁棒基线，并可扩展至大规模图。

Conclusion: KCES 为 GNN 安全提供有理论依据且高效的防御框架。

Abstract: Graph Neural Networks (GNNs) have achieved remarkable success across diverse applications, yet they remain highly vulnerable to adversarial attacks that maliciously perturb graph structure. Existing defenses often lack rigorous theoretical grounding, rely on attack-specific heuristics, or require costly retraining procedures such as adversarial training. To address these limitations, we propose Kernel-Complexity Edge Sanitization (KCES), a training-free and model-agnostic framework for defending against structural attacks. KCES is built upon Graph Kernel Complexity (GKC), a principled metric derived from the graph Gram matrix that appears in a generalization upper bound on the GNN test error. From this bound, we define an edge-specific KC score that quantifies each edge's structural influence via its induced change in GKC. KCES then identifies and prunes high-KC edges, which are empirically enriched with adversarial perturbations under structural attacks, to mitigate their harmful impact. Computationally efficient and scalable, KCES operates as a lightweight preprocessing step without retraining and can be seamlessly integrated with existing defenses. Extensive experiments demonstrate that KCES consistently outperforms representative robust baselines across diverse attack settings and scales effectively to large graphs. Supported by theoretical analysis and extensive empirical validation, KCES provides a principled and efficient framework for securing GNNs. Our code is available at https://github.com/karpning/KCScore.

</details>


### [230] [EFQ-Softmax: Exp-Free Quantization for Softmax](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09721)
*Haohui Han,Yuming Wan,Hongni Wang,Pengcheng Xie,Xiaodong Yan,Runqi You,Wencong Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出EFQ-Softmax，直接用低比特E2M1生成注意力概率，替代传统"先指数后量化"路径，保持模型质量并降低延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低比特注意力将QK^T和PV矩阵乘移至FP8/FP4引擎，但softmax路径仍以高精度计算平移分数指数、生成临时概率块再量化，造成"高精度概率生产者"与"低比特矩阵消费者"之间的精度不匹配。

Method: EFQ-Softmax为每个微缩放块从局部最大值选取仅含指数的scale，将平移分数映射到归一化残差域，用单一仿射规则直接生成非负E2M1概率码；该操作数同时用于P̃V分子更新与P̃1分母更新，保留FlashAttention式行最大值更新、历史重缩放、高精度累加与最终归一化。

Result: Qwen3-8B七任务均值由MXFP4的0.6749提升至0.6773；Qwen3-VL-8B-Instruct九任务均值由0.7826提升至0.8000；WAN2.2在VBench上保持与FP16、MXFP4相当的时间一致性与视觉质量；A5向量单元上，16K至128K序列长度下融合概率生成核的向量阶段延迟平均降低40.33%。

Conclusion: 直接低比特概率生成可替代传统exp-then-quantize路径，在保持端到端模型质量的同时提升效率。

Abstract: Low-bit attention accelerates Transformer inference by moving the $QK^\top$ and $PV$ matrix multiplications to FP8 or FP4 matrix engines. However, the softmax path often evaluates shifted-score exponentials in higher precision, forms a temporary probability block, and quantizes it before low-bit $PV$ multiplication. This exp-then-quantize path creates a mismatch between a high-precision probability producer and a low-bit matrix consumer. We propose EFQ-Softmax (Exp-Free Quantization for Softmax), a low-bit probability-generation method that directly maps shifted attention scores to block-scaled E2M1 operands. For each microscaling block, EFQ-Softmax selects an exponent-only scale from the local maximum, maps the shifted scores to a normalized residual domain, and generates nonnegative E2M1 probability codes using a single affine rule. The resulting operand is used consistently in both the $\widetilde{P}V$ numerator update and the $\widetilde{P}\mathbf{1}$ denominator update. The FlashAttention-style row-maximum update, historical rescaling, high-precision accumulation, and final normalization remain unchanged. We evaluate end-to-end quality on Qwen3-8B, Qwen3-VL-8B-Instruct, and WAN2.2-TI2V-5B, and separately measure kernel-level performance on the A5 vector unit. EFQ-Softmax improves the Qwen3-8B seven-task mean from 0.6749 with MXFP4 to 0.6773 and the Qwen3-VL nine-task mean from 0.7826 to 0.8000. On WAN2.2, it maintains temporal consistency and visual quality comparable to the FP16 and MXFP4 baselines under VBench. On the A5 vector unit, EFQ-Softmax reduces the vector-stage latency of the fused probability-generation kernel by 40.33% on average across sequence lengths from 16K to 128K. These results show that direct low-bit probability generation can replace the conventional exp-then-quantize path while preserving end-to-end model quality.

</details>


### [231] [EEGBind: Detecting Source-Level Interictal Epileptiform Discharges via EEG-Centric Multimodal Binding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09728)
*Muchen Li,Anglin Liu,Xuetian Gao,Ruijian Xu,Jintai Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 EEGBind，一种以 EEG 为核心的多模态绑定框架，用于五类发作间期癫痫样放电（IED）的源级分类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 源级 IED 分析对术前评估与治疗方案制定具有重要意义，需要判断 IED 活动来自哪一临床脑区。但短时 EEG 窗口中的源区证据微弱、不完整，并受被试差异、类别不平衡与多模态上下文不完善的影响，任务困难。

Method: EEGBind 将 EEG 视为主要模态，以 EEG 中心表征为核心，绑定同步的视频上下文特征，把视频作为辅助证据而非早期或过强的多模态融合，以避免扰动对源区敏感的 EEG 表征；同时引入视角一致的修复阶段以提升隐藏集鲁棒性，并保持已学到的源类边界。

Result: 在 NeuroMM 2026 Grand Challenge Track 3 NMM-Source-IED 基准上，EEGBind 取得 0.8395 的加权 F1，优于多个强竞争者。

Conclusion: 结果支持「以 EEG 为中心的多模态绑定」是源级 IED 分类的实用策略，代码已开源。

Abstract: Source-level analysis of interictal epileptiform discharges (IEDs) is relevant to presurgical evaluation and treatment planning because it helps characterize where epileptiform activity is likely to arise. Beyond detecting whether an IED is present, this setting requires assigning IED-positive activity to clinically meaningful brain-region categories. This setting is challenging because source-region evidence in short electroencephalography (EEG) windows can be subtle, partial, and affected by subject variability, class imbalance, and imperfect multimodal context. We present EEGBind, an EEG-centric multimodal binding framework for five-class source-level IED classification. EEGBind treats EEG as the primary modality and binds synchronized video-context features around an EEG-centric representation. Instead of relying on early or overly strong multimodal fusion, which may perturb the source-sensitive EEG representation, EEGBind uses video context as auxiliary evidence for robust classification. A view-consistent repair stage is further used to improve hidden-set robustness while preserving the learned source-class boundary. On the NeuroMM 2026 Grand Challenge Track 3 NMM-Source-IED benchmark, EEGBind achieves 0.8395 on weighted-F1 and outperforms strong competitors. These results support EEG-centric multimodal binding as a practical strategy for source-level IED classification. The open-source code is available at https://github.com/HKUSTGZ-ML4Health-Lab/NeuroMM2026_IED_Detection.

</details>


### [232] [Fine-Tuning a KV Cache Concatenation-Aware Model or Recomputing KV Caches? Why Not Both?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09768)
*Fumihiko Tachibana,Daisuke Miyashita,Jun Deguchi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文针对RAG系统中长上下文导致的TTFT过高与KV缓存复用后质量下降问题，提出结合KV缓存拼接感知微调与选择性重算KV缓存的方案，在RULER基准上124k token输入场景提升9.7分并将TTFT降低80%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RAG系统需拼接大量检索片段作为上下文，导致输入长度大增、prefill负载上升、TTFT变长。已有复用预计算KV缓存的方法虽能降低TTFT，但在超长上下文下响应质量是否保持尚不明确。

Method: 提出组合方法：(i) 在微调模型时考虑KV缓存拼接的影响；(ii) 选择性地重算一部分KV缓存。

Result: 在RULER基准上，124k token输入时RULER分数比仅重算KV缓存的基线提升9.7分；TTFT相比全注意力降低80%。

Conclusion: 通过KV缓存拼接感知微调与选择性KV缓存重算相结合，可在长上下文输入下同时提升准确率与推理效率。

Abstract: In Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) systems, a large number of retrieved chunks are concatenated to form the input context so that users can receive high-quality responses based on external knowledge. As a result, the input context length increases substantially, leading to a larger prefill workload and, in turn, a longer time to first token (TTFT). While previous works that reuse precomputed key-value (KV) caches effectively reduce TTFT for long-context inputs, it remains unclear whether response quality is preserved when the input context becomes very long. In this paper, we propose a combined approach that (i) fine-tunes the model while taking KV cache concatenation into account and (ii) selectively recomputes a subset of the KV caches. By applying both techniques, we demonstrate improved accuracy for long-context inputs. Experiments on the RULER benchmark show that, for a 124k-token input, our method improves the RULER score by 9.7 point over the baseline that recomputes KV caches only. Moreover, TTFT is reduced by 80% compared with full attention.

</details>


### [233] [BRACE: Anchored Bellman-Residual Correction for Stale Critics in Asynchronous RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09783)
*Guanqun Zhao,Zijun Xie,Binbin Zheng,Jiafeng Lu,Enlei Gong,Zeyu Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 BRACE，用锚定的 Bellman 残差校正缓解异步强化学习中的策略滞后偏差，在长程智能体任务上兼顾效率与稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 异步强化学习是扩展语言模型训练的标准方式，但策略滞后使批评者偏向过时的行为策略。已有异步 LLM 训练工作只校正演员而忽略该偏差；经典 RL 的离策略价值校正又因短校正视野缺少奖励、长视野重要性比率随轨迹长度指数漂移，无法推广到长程智能体任务。

Method: 提出 BRACE（锚定的 Bellman 残差校正）。将校正视野限制在策略 token 的前缀内，并在其后锚定一个恒定权重的蒙特卡洛尾部，从而把策略校正与奖励传播解耦。

Result: 在 BrowseComp-Plus 上 mean@1 比最强基线提升 2.4%；每步训练速度是同步训练的 2.46 倍；在离策略 50 次更新后仍保持稳定。

Conclusion: BRACE 有效缓解异步 LLM 强化学习中的陈旧价值偏差，在长程智能体任务上同时实现性能、效率与稳定性的提升。

Abstract: Asynchronous reinforcement learning has become the standard way to scale training for language models, but the resulting policy lag biases the critic toward the stale behavior policy. Existing work on asynchronous LLM training corrects the actor and leaves this bias unaddressed, while the off-policy value correction of classical RL does not carry over to long-horizon agentic tasks, since a short correction horizon leaves the regression target free of the reward and a long one lets the product of importance ratios drift exponentially with the trajectory length. We propose BRACE, an anchored Bellman-residual correction for stale value models. BRACE bounds the correction horizon to a prefix of policy tokens and anchors a constant-weight Monte-Carlo tail beyond it, which separates policy correction from reward propagation. BRACE improves mean@1 on BrowseComp-Plus by $2.4\%$ over the strongest baseline, runs $2.46\times$ faster per step than synchronous training, and remains stable $50$ updates off-policy.

</details>


### [234] [NEXUS-MI: Communication-Aware Federated Personalization for Gateway-Coordinated Motor-Imagery Brain-Computer Interfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09786)
*Daniel Adu Worae,Aarthy Nagarajan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出NEXUS-MI，一个网关协调的联邦个性化框架，将同步视为学习与通信耦合控制问题，用于EEG运动想象脑机接口。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: EEG运动想象脑机接口在受试者和会话间差异大，有限校准数据下个性化困难；联邦学习可利用共享表征而无需集中原始EEG，但现有联邦MI研究大多假设规则同步，忽视了通信链路的异质性。

Method: 原始EEG与分类头保留在本地，边缘协调器维护共享主干。通过离线回放评估，使用BCICIV-2a（9人4类）和OpenBMI（54人2类）。会话1用于主干学习，会话2用于有限校准个性化与留出测试。设置理想链路参考与六种异构链路策略，刻画网关参与、缓冲、过期更新准入与主干下载控制，固定延迟更新处理，对比非自适应与通信感知同步；采用Holm校正的受试者级配对比较，并用层次自助法评估五种匹配实现下的稳健性。

Result: 通信感知协调在两个数据集上使服务器到客户端的主干流量减少约42%，但队列级准确率差异较小且依赖具体实现。队列平均还掩盖了受试者级脆弱性，BCICIV-2a上相对理想链路参考的损失可达约12个百分点。

Conclusion: 网关同步应作为联邦MI个性化中的显式设计变量，需联合评估个性化准确率、通信成本、更新新鲜度与受试者级可靠性。

Abstract: Electroencephalography (EEG)-based motor-imagery brain-computer interfaces (MI-BCIs) vary across subjects and sessions, complicating personalization from limited calibration data. Federated learning can exploit shared representations without centralizing raw EEG, but existing federated MI studies largely assume regular synchronization. We introduce NEXUS-MI, a gateway-coordinated federated personalization framework that treats synchronization as a coupled learning-and-communication control problem. Raw EEG and classifier heads remain local, while an edge coordinator maintains the shared backbone. We evaluate NEXUS-MI through offline replay using BCI Competition IV Dataset 2a (BCICIV-2a; 9 subjects, 4 classes) and OpenBMI (54 subjects, 2 classes). Session 1 supports backbone learning, and Session 2 provides limited-calibration personalization and held-out testing. An ideal-link reference and six heterogeneous-link policies characterize gateway participation, buffering, stale-update admission, and backbone-download control. The principal comparison holds delayed-update handling fixed while contrasting non-adaptive and communication-aware synchronization. Paired subject-level comparisons use Holm adjustment, and robustness across five matched realizations is assessed by hierarchical bootstrap. Communication-aware coordination reduced server-to-client backbone traffic by approximately 42% on both datasets, while cohort-level accuracy differences were small and realization-dependent. Cohort averages also concealed subject-level vulnerability, with losses reaching approximately 12 percentage points on BCICIV-2a relative to the ideal-link reference. These findings establish gateway synchronization as an explicit design variable in federated MI personalization and motivate joint evaluation of personalized accuracy, communication cost, update freshness, and subject-level reliability.

</details>


### [235] [Evaluating Model Retraining under Drift: Paired Comparisons of Cumulative Subgroup Disparity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09788)
*Aaron Ceross*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 比较三种分类器重训练策略对亚组误差累积差异的影响，发现策略比较高度依赖评估群体与加权方式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已部署分类器在何时重训练需要评估更新序列（含更新间隔期）中的亚组错误率，但现有做法缺乏对完整调度、损失触发与亚组差距触发等策略在同一观测与延迟标签下的系统比较。

Method: 在同一观测与延迟标签下，比较完整调度、损失触发、亚组差距触发三种策略与保留初始模型；对真阳率与假阳率分别以部署窗口内绝对亚组差距的配对差值之和为结果指标。通过模拟、动作记录与替代调度的群体评估考察测量与重训练行为的影响，并进行每组400条新轨迹的跟进样本（两种漂移情形）及美国社区调查回放的探索性分析。

Result: 三种策略的平均累积差异均低于保留初始模型，相当于每窗口平均差距降低0.04至0.88个百分点；用未更新模型对照已知生成分布保持了所有均值方向，但有限窗口与群体比较仅在69%至92%的轨迹上就更新是否增加、降低或不变达成一致。亚组特定漂移下，真阳率差距较小伴随两组敏感度均较低。在探索性回放中，个体加权使三个种族假阳率均值比较方向全部反转，但预测与动作不变，且三个加权区间均含零。

Conclusion: 策略比较需要组别特异错误率、动作分布与明确的评估群体，并需与平均差异一并报告；本分析为非确证性，共享回放要求观测与策略无关且延迟后标签完整。

Abstract: Choosing when to retrain a deployed classifier requires assessing subgroup error rates across the sequence of models used, including periods between updates. We compare complete scheduled, loss-triggered, and subgroup-gap-triggered policies with retaining the initial model on the same observations and delayed labels. For true-positive and false-positive rates separately, the outcome is the paired difference in absolute subgroup gaps summed over deployment windows. Population evaluation in simulation, action records, and alternative schedules assess how measurement and retraining behaviour affect these comparisons. In a follow-up sample of 400 new trajectories per condition across two simulated drift regimes, all three policies had lower mean cumulative disparity, equivalent to reductions of 0.04 to 0.88 percentage points in the average gap per window. Evaluating the unchanged models against the known generating distributions preserved all mean directions, but finite-window and population comparisons agreed on whether updating increased, reduced or left cumulative disparity unchanged in 69 to 92 percent of trajectories. Under subgroup-specific drift, smaller true-positive-rate gaps accompanied lower sensitivity in both groups. In an exploratory American Community Survey replay, person weighting reversed all three race false-positive-rate mean comparisons without changing predictions or actions; all three weighted intervals included zero. Policy comparisons require group-specific rates, action distributions, and an explicit evaluation population alongside mean disparity. These analyses are non-confirmatory. Shared replay requires policy-independent observations and complete labels after the specified delay.

</details>


### [236] [Privacy-Preserving Split Learning for Federated LLM Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09794)
*Heng Jin,Chaoyu Zhang,Hexuan Yu,Wenjing Lou,Y. Thomas Hou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文针对拆分学习与联邦学习结合的LLM微调场景中传输激活值泄露输入数据的隐私悖论，提出可学习的“混淆-恢复”方案，在有限效用损失与系统开销下实现强隐私保护。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在真实部署中，参与者往往无法本地持有完整模型（模型归所有者专有或本地算力不足），因此采用拆分学习（SL）将模型分片；当数据又分布在多个机构且需隐私保护时，进一步结合联邦学习（FL）。但该联合设置下客户端需向服务器传输中间激活值，而LLM的自回归特性使得这些激活值会泄露原始输入，形成固有隐私悖论，且现有基于扰动的防御在此场景下根本无效。

Method: 提出一种可学习的混淆-恢复（obfuscate-and-recover）机制：在客户端对传输的中间激活进行混淆以保护私有数据集，同时在服务器侧能够恢复/训练出可独立部署的模型，从而在隐私与可用性之间取得平衡。

Result: 实验表明，该方法在实现强隐私保护的同时仅带来适度的效用损失和系统开销，使基于拆分的联邦LLM微调在实际中可行。

Conclusion: 通过学习式混淆-恢复方案，可有效缓解SL+FL联合LLM微调中激活值泄露输入的问题，兼顾隐私、效用与效率，为该类部署提供了实用路径。

Abstract: Fine-tuning large language models (LLMs) on domain-specific data is essential for downstream adaptation. In many deployments, a participant cannot hold the complete model locally. This happens because the model owner keeps the full model proprietary, or because the participant lacks sufficient compute resources. Split Learning (SL) addresses this by partitioning the model between the participant and a server so that only a small portion runs locally. When the underlying data is additionally distributed across multiple institutions with privacy requirements, Federated Learning (FL) further enables collaborative training across participants by sharing only model updates instead of raw data. In this combined setting, each client transmits intermediate activations to the server, and for LLM fine-tuning, this exchange poses an inherent privacy paradox. The autoregressive nature of LLMs causes the transmitted activations to leak the input, and existing perturbation-based defenses are fundamentally ineffective in this setting. We address this leakage through a learned obfuscate-and-recover scheme that protects participants' private datasets while still allowing an independently deployable model to be trained on the server side. Experiments demonstrate that our approach achieves strong privacy protection with modest utility loss and system overhead, making split-based federated LLM fine-tuning practically viable.

</details>


### [237] [A practical DIRECT-type algorithm for medium-scale black-box global optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09796)
*Linas Stripinis,Remigijus Paulavičius*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 X-DTC-GL，一种融合动态划分与混合策略的 DIRECT 型确定性全局优化算法，缓解高维下收敛慢的问题，在四个基准套件上显著优于现有 DIRECT 类方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DIRECT 算法虽通用且兼顾探索与利用，但主要适用于低维问题，随维度升高收敛变慢，限制其在复杂优化任务中的应用。

Method: 引入动态划分：基于局部一维代理模型自适应细化搜索空间，快速细分有潜力的超矩形；引入混合策略：选择性使用爬山法，对代理模型识别出的有潜力区域进行开发。

Result: 在四个基准套件上，可解性提升约 12%，解质量提升约 27%；性能剖面分析显示在最多约 40% 实例上收敛最快，约 17% 问题上运行时间最佳，整体执行时间具竞争力。

Conclusion: X-DTC-GL 在基于划分的框架内提升性能，增强其在先进黑盒优化中的竞争力。

Abstract: The DIRECT algorithm is a deterministic global optimization method known for its versatility and balanced exploration-exploitation strategy. However, DIRECT-type algorithms are primarily effective for low-dimensional problems and often exhibit slow convergence as dimensionality increases, limiting their applicability to more complex optimization tasks. To address this limitation, this paper introduces X-DTC-GL, a novel DIRECT-type algorithm that incorporates dynamic partitioning and hybridization techniques. The dynamic partitioning approach adaptively refines the search space based on local one-dimensional surrogate models, enabling rapid subdivision of promising hyper-rectangles. The hybridization strategy selectively employs a hill-climbing method to exploit promising regions identified by the surrogate models. Extensive experiments on four diverse benchmark suites demonstrate that X-DTC-GL significantly outperforms existing DIRECT-type baselines, achieving improvements of ~12% in solvability and ~27% in solution quality. Performance-profile analyses indicate the fastest convergence on up to ~40% of instances, the best runtime performance on ~17% of problems, and competitive overall execution times. By improving performance within the partition-based framework, these advances strengthen the algorithm's competitiveness in state-of-the-art black-box optimization.

</details>


### [238] [Online Inverse Integer Linear Optimization via Small-Gradient Skipping: Constant Regret and Finite Mistakes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09809)
*Akira Kitaoka*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出 Small-Gradient Skipping (SGS) 机制，通过跳过无错误轮次的更新，改进了在线逆线性优化中遗憾界的维度依赖并避免计算重心。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线逆线性优化中，一般情形下遗憾上界 O(d log T) 与下界 Ω(d) 之间存在未解决的 log T 间隙；当行动集为 M-convex 时虽可达 O(d log d)，但需每轮计算重心，计算代价高。论文旨在消除该间隙并降低计算复杂度。

Method: 提出 Small-Gradient Skipping (SGS) 机制：在正确行动与其他候选一致分离的情形下，跳过未犯错误轮次的更新。将该机制应用于在线梯度下降、在线牛顿法 (online Newton step) 和 MetaGrad。

Result: 三种算法的错误次数均被界定为与 T 无关的量；对结合 SGS 的在线牛顿法和 MetaGrad，当前向问题为整数线性规划时，遗憾的维度依赖降为 O(d^2)，即去除了 log T 因子；当行动集为 M-convex 时，能在不计算重心的情况下高效界定遗憾。

Conclusion: SGS 机制有效消除了在线逆线性优化中的 log T 间隙，并避免了 M-convex 情形下计算重心的开销，为更高效率的算法提供了新思路。

Abstract: In online inverse linear optimization, the learner predicts a weight at each round, observes the optimal action of the agent, and updates its prediction. In the general setting, the gap of $\log T$ between the regret upper bound $O(d \log T)$ and the lower bound $Ω(d)$ is unresolved (here $T$ is the total number of rounds and $d$ is the dimension). When the action set is M-convex, the regret is known to be bounded by $O(d \log d)$, but the method attaining it computes a center of gravity at every round. This paper therefore proposes Small-Gradient Skipping (SGS), a mechanism that skips the update at rounds without a mistake in the case where the correct action is uniformly separated from the other candidates, and applies it to online gradient descent, the online Newton step, and MetaGrad. The number of mistakes is then bounded, for all three, by a quantity independent of $T$; and for the online Newton step and for MetaGrad with SGS, the dimension dependence of the regret becomes $O(d^2)$ when the forward problem is an integer linear program, that is, the factor $\log T$ is removed. Moreover, when the action set is M-convex, the regret is bounded efficiently without computing a center of gravity.

</details>


### [239] [In Medical Claims Data, Enhancing Predictive Performance for Major Adverse Cardiovascular Events Using Cross Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09824)
*Yuhei Fujioka,Daitaro Misawa,Tatsuyoshi Ikenoue,Shingo Fukuma*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究利用交叉注意力机制整合医疗理赔数据中的诊断与治疗关系，提升主要不良心血管事件（MACE）的预测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医疗理赔数据包含费用与临床信息，可构建大型数据库用于医学研究，但其临床信息多为非结构化，限制了综合分析应用；而MACE预测模型对临床实践指南至关重要，因此需要更有效地表征这类数据。

Method: 采用交叉注意力机制，对诊断与治疗之间的关系进行有效加权，从而生成更具代表性的特征用于MACE预测。

Result: 所提交叉注意力模型的ROC-AUC为0.7720，高于传统动脉粥样硬化性心血管疾病模型、LightGBM以及基于自注意力的模型等基准模型。

Conclusion: 通过交叉注意力机制整合医疗理赔数据的临床结构，能显著提升预测模型的性能。

Abstract: Medical claims data comprise the financial details, including the expenses and billing information, as well as the clinical information, such as the diagnoses and treatments, of patients visiting medical facilities. Recently, it has been acknowledged that large databases can be constructed from medical claims data for medical research purposes. However, the clinical information within these datasets is often medically unstructured, limiting its application in comprehensive analyses. This study enhances predictive model performance for major adverse cardiovascular events (MACE), a leading cause of death worldwide. Models that predict MACE are crucial to clinical practice guidelines. We utilize a cross-attention mechanism to develop a method that effectively weights the relationships between diagnoses and treatments. Effectively repre- senting the clinical information contained in medical claims data, this approach generates more representative features for predicting MACE. The ROC-AUC score of our proposed cross-attention-based model was 0.7720, higher than other benchmark models including the conventional atherosclerotic cardiovascular disease model, the light gradient boosting machine, and a self-attention-based model. These results indicate that integrating the clinical structure of medical claims data using a cross-attention mechanism significantly enhances the performance of predictive models.

</details>


### [240] [TempTPI: Informer-Based trajectory prediction for maritime vessels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09840)
*Kevin Ferneding,Veronika Lietavcova,Aleksandra M. Blachowiak,Peder Heiselberg*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出TempTPI框架，结合Informer编码器与多通道时间编码，用于海事船舶长期轨迹预测，在丹麦水域AIS数据上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 准确的长期船舶轨迹预测对海上安全和物流效率至关重要。现有深度学习模型（尤其是Transformer）处理AIS数据时面临自注意力二次计算复杂度和长期预测精度下降的问题。

Method: 提出TempTPI框架：以Informer为基础编码器，采用ProbSparse稀疏自注意力机制降低计算开销并聚焦关键依赖；同时引入多通道时间编码机制，利用类傅里叶频率展开捕捉船舶行为中的周期性模式（小时、日、季节）。

Result: 在丹麦水域AIS数据上，与最先进的TPTrans架构对比，TempTPI在1至5小时多个预测窗口上均持续优于现有方法；在5小时预测窗口下MSE改善达55%。

Conclusion: TempTPI为长程海上态势感知提供了一种鲁棒且高效的轨迹预测解决方案。

Abstract: Accurate long-term trajectory prediction for maritime vessels is essential for safety and logistical efficiency. While deep learning models, particularly Transformers, have shown promise in processing Automatic Identification System (AIS) data, they often struggle with the quadratic computational complexity of self-attention and the loss of accuracy over extended forecasting horizons. This study proposes TempTPI, a novel prediction framework that integrates an Informer-based encoder with a multi-channel temporal encoding mechanism. The Informer architecture leverages a ProbSparse self-attention mechanism to reduce computational overhead and focus on the most significant dependencies, while the temporal encoder utilizes Fourier-like frequency expansions to capture cyclic patterns (hourly, daily, and seasonal) in vessel behavior. We evaluate our model against the state-of-the-art TPTrans architecture using AIS data from Danish waters. Experimental results demonstrate that TempTPI consistently outperforms existing methods across prediction windows of 1 to 5 hours. Notably, at a 5-hour horizon, the proposed model achieves a 55% improvement in Mean Squared Error (MSE), offering a robust solution for long-range maritime situational awareness.

</details>


### [241] [Exact Degeneracy Under Balanced k-Shot Sampling:Consequences for Small-Sample Discriminant Analysis on LLM Embeddings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09860)
*Lingxiao Qu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文揭示平衡k-shot采样会使KLPCDA类判别估计器产生精确可证的退化，并检验其修复框架在LLM嵌入少样本文本分类上的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 平衡k-shot采样（每类恰好k个标注样本）是小样本学习的常见设置，但作者关注其对KLPCDA这类小样本判别估计器是否引入了结构性的、可证明的退化，而非仅仅是不适定问题；同时想验证源自像素、振动信号和基因表达数据的既有指导是否能推广到LLM文本嵌入空间。

Method: 理论分析：证明在平衡采样下KLPCDA的类内散度算子恰为缩放的正交投影算子，并推导七个变体的闭式后果（两个变体信号特征值完全相等、第三个目标函数为空）。实验：在冻结句子嵌入与仅解码器生成模型的残差流激活上验证；提出公式内平局打破规则修复变体；在四个数据集、三种嵌入规模（n远小于d，d最高4096）和三种基线（SetFit、LoRA、上下文学习）上评估少样本文本分类，并用三个几何可分性度量检验嵌入质量差异的原因。

Result: 理论上确认了退化完全由估计器构造导致，与数据集无关；公式内平局打破可修复两个可修复变体，恢复程度受类别数影响（少类数据集上残差子空间约束的代价是多类数据集的5倍，p=0.000001）。实验中，经过适当交叉验证的逻辑回归探针在四个数据集中的三个、所有嵌入规模上均优于所有KLPCDA变体；三个几何可分性度量都无法解释某高维解码器嵌入模型不如更小双向编码器的现象，排除了各向异性的解释；该差距主要是估计效率效应而非永久上限，当支持集从k≤10增至k=30-50时差距缩小超过80%（p=0.00195）。

Conclusion: 平衡k-shot采样对KLPCDA引起的退化是构造性的、可证明的；修复后的框架虽有效，但基于像素、振动信号和基因表达数据的经验指导不能直接推广到LLM嵌入的特征空间，少样本LLM分类中简单交叉验证的逻辑回归仍是更强基线，解码器嵌入的劣势源于估计效率不足而非固定上限。

Abstract: Balanced k-shot sampling draws exactly k labeled examples per class. We show that it induces an exact, provable degeneracy in a family of small-sample discriminant estimators. Under balanced sampling, the within-class scatter operator of Kernelized Linear Principal Component Discriminant Analysis (KLPCDA) is not merely rank-deficient but exactly a scaled orthogonal projector. We derive the consequences in closed form: two of KLPCDA's seven variants have every signal eigenvalue exactly equal, so their eigenvector selection criterion is provably indifferent rather than ill-conditioned, and a third has a provably void objective. This follows from the estimators' construction, not any dataset; we confirm it on frozen sentence embeddings and, separately, on residual-stream activations from a decoder-only generative model. An in-formula tie-break repairs the two repairable variants, with recovery gated by class count: the residual subspace constraint costs 5x more on few-class than many-class datasets (p=0.000001).
  We then evaluate the repaired framework on few-shot text classification on frozen LLM embeddings (n much smaller than d, up to 4096), across four datasets, three embedding sizes, and three trained baselines (SetFit, LoRA, in-context learning). A properly cross-validated logistic-regression probe still beats every KLPCDA variant on three of four datasets, at every embedding size; guidance carried from pixel, vibration-signal, and gene-expression data does not directly generalize to this feature space. Three independent geometric separability metrics fail to explain why one high-dimensional decoder-based embedding model underperforms smaller bidirectional encoders, ruling out anisotropy; the gap is substantially an estimation-efficiency effect, not a permanent ceiling, closing by more than 80% when the support set grows from k<=10 to k=30-50 (p=0.00195, both many-class datasets).

</details>


### [242] [Forward-Free LLM Depth Pruning via Weight Redundancy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09883)
*Vincent-Daniel Yun,Woosang Lim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出免前向传播的深度剪枝方法WRP，通过检查点权重估计层间冗余来筛选Transformer块。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度剪枝通过移除整个Transformer块降低LLM推理成本。基于激活的方法需要校准数据前向传播，而现有免前向方法仅单独评估每个块，未度量块间相似性。

Method: WRP比较各层的注意力输出权重与MLP下投影权重，结合成对相似性与相对投影尺度信息，构建全对相似度矩阵，据此进行层分组与块选择。

Result: 在多种剪枝设置、模型系列和下游任务中，WRP持续优于现有免前向幅度剪枝方法，并接近基于激活方法的性能。

Conclusion: WRP提供了一种无需校准数据和前向传播的高效深度剪枝方案，性能接近激活方法。

Abstract: Depth pruning reduces large language model (LLM) inference cost by removing complete Transformer blocks. Activation-based methods collect hidden states through forward passes on calibration data, while existing forward-free methods score each Transformer block separately without measuring similarity between blocks. We propose Weight-Redundancy Pruning (WRP), a forward-free depth-pruning method that estimates inter-layer redundancy from checkpoint weights to select blocks without calibration data or model forward passes. WRP compares attention output and MLP down-projection weights across layers and combines their pairwise similarities with relative projection-scale information. The resulting all-pairs similarity matrix guides layer grouping and block selection. Across multiple pruning settings, model families, and downstream tasks, WRP consistently outperforms existing forward-free magnitude pruning and approaches the performance of activation-based methods.

</details>


### [243] [ProMeta: Few-shot PROTAC-targeted degradation prediction across E3 ligases](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09891)
*Yuansheng Liu,Yufei Ye,Tao Tang,Jiawei Luo,Wen Tao,Xiao Luo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ProMeta将跨E3连接酶的PROTAC降解活性预测重构为小样本元学习问题，用原型图神经网络实现跨连接酶泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有PROTAC降解活性预测方法受限于数据稀缺和E3连接酶间的数据不平衡，标注数据集中于CRBN、VHL等少数连接酶，难以泛化到研究不足的连接酶，而扩展靶向降解剂设计空间需要稳健的跨连接酶泛化能力。

Method: 将跨E3连接酶的PROTAC降解活性预测重构为小样本元学习问题；提出ProMeta，一种基于原型的图神经网络，在源E3任务上进行情景式元学习训练，在留出目标E3任务上通过支持集条件化推理评估；推理时不更新编码器，仅从少量目标连接酶支持样本动态估计类原型。

Result: 在CRBN-to-VHL基准上，K=2、Q=3时AUROC为0.796，K=2、Q=5时为0.883，较对应监督GNN基线分别提升19.9%和6.8%；反向VHL-to-CRBN迁移在同协议下AUROC为0.702（K=2, Q=3）和0.821（K=2, Q=5），验证了双向适用性，同时揭示方向与数据机制依赖性。

Conclusion: ProMeta是所评估支持/查询协议下跨连接酶小样本预测的实用框架，支持在极少标注数据下实现稳健的跨E3连接酶泛化，有助于提升计算PROTAC发现的实用性。

Abstract: Proteolysis-targeting chimeras (PROTACs) have emerged as a transformative therapeutic strategy that selectively degrades historically ''undruggable'' targets via the ubiquitin-proteasome system. Despite growing efforts to develop computational predictors of PROTAC degradation activity, existing supervised approaches remain severely challenged by data scarcity and imbalance across E3 ligases, limiting their ability to generalize beyond well-studied ligase contexts. In practice, labeled data are heavily concentrated on a few ligases (e.g., CRBN and VHL), while the majority of E3 ligases remain underexplored yet are critical for expanding the design space of targeted degraders. Developing methods that enable robust cross-ligase generalization with minimal labeled data is therefore essential for improving the practical utility of computational PROTAC discovery. We reformulate PROTAC degradation activity prediction across E3 ligases as a few-shot meta-learning problem and present ProMeta, a prototype-based graph neural network trained through episodic meta-learning on source-E3 tasks and evaluated on held-out target-E3 tasks through support-conditioned inference. ProMeta performs inference without updating the encoder by dynamically estimating class prototypes from minimal target-ligase support samples. On the CRBN-to-VHL benchmark, ProMeta achieves AUROC values of 0.796 under K=2, Q=3 and 0.883 under K=2, Q=5, improving by 19.9% and 6.8%, respectively, over the corresponding supervised GNN baseline. Reverse VHL-to-CRBN transfer under the same protocol yielded AUROC values of 0.702 (K=2, Q=3) and 0.821 (K=2, Q=5), confirming bidirectional applicability while revealing direction and data-regime dependence. Together, these results support ProMeta as a practical framework for cross-ligase few-shot prediction under the evaluated support/query protocols.

</details>


### [244] [Strangers to Themselves: What Language Models Say About Themselves Is Generic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09899)
*Phil Blandfort,Urja Pawar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文将语言模型的"自我认知"转化为预测测试，发现模型对自身行为的直接自我报告预测力很弱（r=+0.04），其预测能力主要来自对"通用AI助手"的一般性理论而非关于自身的特权知识，且第一人称视角会带来美化偏向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型能流利描述自己在压力下是否会屈服、滥用工具或说谎，但人们不清楚这种描述究竟反映的是"该模型自身"的知识，还是关于AI助手的泛化理论。作者希望检验模型的自我报告是否具备真正的"自我知识"价值。

Method: 在九项行为评估中，测量模型在不同条件下的实际行为，让其预测自身行为发生率，并设置去除"自我"、改为询问"通用有能力的AI智能体"或询问其他模型的对照条件；同时考察模型规模的影响、第一人称框架效应，以及用模型自身行为记录进行微调的效果。

Result: (i) 直接自我报告预测力弱（r=+0.04），展示具体题目后仅升至+0.24；而关于"通用AI智能体"的同样问题可达+0.28，其他模型对自身的回答对目标模型的预测力不亚于其自身回答。(ii) 前沿规模未明显改变该模式，预测增益并非自我特异的。(iii) 第一人称框架有稳定效应：报告向美化方向偏移，低估有害行为。(iv) 用自身行为记录微调可教会狭窄的自我预测，但同时改变了被预测的行为，且增益无法广泛迁移。

Conclusion: 询问模型"它会怎么做"，主要揭示的是关于AI助手的一般性理论与一种对自身有利的偏向，而非对该模型的特权性知识；自我报告不宜作为模型自我认知的可靠依据。

Abstract: Language models can fluently describe how they would behave: whether they would cave to pushback, misuse a tool, or lie under pressure. Is that description actually about the model speaking? We turn self-knowledge into a prediction test. Across nine behavioral evaluations, we measure how a model behaves under different conditions, ask it to predict those rates, and compare its predictions with controls that remove the self from the question. We find that: (i) Direct self-report is weak (r = +0.04), and even showing the model the exact items only raises prediction to +0.24. Crucially, the same item-informed question about "capable AI agents in general" does just as well (+0.28), while other models' answers about themselves predict the target model at least as well as its own. (ii) Frontier scale does not detectably change this pattern: any gains in prediction are not self-specific, and are consistent with a better theory of how AI assistants behave rather than better self-knowledge. (iii) First-person framing does have one robust effect: it shifts reports in the flattering direction, understating harmful behavior relative to the same question about a generic agent. (iv) Finetuning on a model's own behavioral record can teach narrow self-predictions, but it also changes the behavior being predicted and the gains do not transfer broadly. The practical implication is simple: asking a model what it would do mostly reveals a theory of AI assistants in general, plus a favorable bias, rather than privileged knowledge of that model.

</details>


### [245] [Beyond Conventional Federated Learning via High-Order Regularization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09904)
*Alireza Kabgani,Masoud Ahookhosh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: HiFedProx 用尺度匹配的幂型正则项（指数 p≥2）替代 FedProx 的二次惩罚，以压缩联邦学习中客户端位移差异；实验表明 p=5–7 在应力条件下表现最佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦客户端在多步本地优化后返回的参数位移幅度差异很大。FedProx 的二次正则随位移线性增长，对普通位移与异常大位移之间的对比控制有限，难以有效抑制位移差异。

Method: 提出 HiFedProx，将二次惩罚替换为由 p≥2 索引的尺度匹配幂型正则项；所有幂次在参考位移 R 处具有相同的正则梯度幅值，p>2 时在 R 以下响应更弱、R 以上响应更强。结合有限预算的随机客户端优化与同小批量 Armijo 回溯线搜索。

Result: 在冻结的 60 写作者 FEMNIST 子集上做配对五种子实验，对 p∈{2,…,8} 进行公共参数研究：干净训练性能相近，但复合应力下显著提升；中等与严重应力最低损失分别出现在 p=7 和 p=6，相对 p=2 提升 11.44% 与 23.16%。位移尾部比率随 p 至 8 持续下降，但预测性能在中间范围达峰，且 Armijo 试次成本随 p 增加。

Conclusion: 指数应被校准而非一味增大；本实验中 p=5–7 是最有用的取值范围，兼顾位移差异压缩与预测性能及计算成本。

Abstract: Federated clients that perform several local optimization steps can return parameter displacements with widely different magnitudes. The quadratic regularization of FedProx grows linearly with displacement and therefore offers limited control over the contrast between ordinary and unusually large client movements. We here introduce HiFedProx, which replaces the quadratic penalty with a scale-matched power-type regularizer indexed by $p\geq2$. All powers have the same regularization-gradient magnitude at a reference displacement $R$, while every $p>2$ gives a weaker response below $R$ and a stronger response above it. An exact affine reference calculation shows that increasing $p$ compresses relative displacement disparities, although very large powers approach fixed-radius behavior and increase local curvature. HiFedProx combines this geometry with finite-budget stochastic client optimization and same-minibatch Armijo backtracking. In paired five-seed experiments on a frozen 60-writer FEMNIST subset, a common-parameter study over $p\in\{2,3,4,5,6,7,8\}$ shows similar clean-training performance but substantial gains under composite stress. The lowest moderate- and severe-stress losses occur at $p=7$ and $p=6$, improving over $p=2$ by $11.44\%$ and $23.16\%$, respectively. Although displacement-tail ratios continue to decrease through $p=8$, predictive performance peaks in an intermediate range and Armijo trial cost increases with $p$.
